  .field { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 8px; }
  .field-error { grid-column: 1; margin-top: 0; }
  .control-wrap { max-width: none; }
}


/* Reflow keeps controls reachable without fixed-height overlays. */
nav a[aria-current="location"] { background: var(--badge); font-weight: 600; }
nav a:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--focus); outline-offset: 2px; }
.app-header > div, .actions > *, .field > * { min-width: 0; }
.actions, .app-header { flex-wrap: wrap; }
button, .effect-summary { overflow-wrap: anywhere; white-space: normal; }
@media (max-width: 720px), (max-height: 600px) {

  .effect-summary { flex-basis: 100%; }

}

    animation: none !important; transition: none !important; scroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }
}

===== BEGIN FILE: renderer/settings.js =====
'use strict';

const state = {
  baseline: {},
  defaults: {},
  configuration: {},
  validationGeneration: 0,
  values: {},
  schema: [],
  errors: {},
  effects: [],
  validationTimer: null,
  saving: false,
  validating: false,
};

const form = document.getElementById('settings-form');
const sectionsHost = document.getElementById('settings-sections');
const nav = document.getElementById('settings-nav');
const template = document.getElementById('section-template');
const saveButton = document.getElementById('save');
const resetButton = document.getElementById('reset');
const message = document.getElementById('form-message');
const dirtyBadge = document.getElementById('dirty-badge');
const effectSummary = document.getElementById('effect-summary');

function equal(a, b) {
  return JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
}

function isDirty() {
  return !equal(state.values, state.baseline);
}

function collectPatch() {
  const patch = {};
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(state.values)) {
    if (!equal(value, state.baseline[key])) patch[key] = value;
  }
  return patch;
}

function fieldForKey(key) {
  for (const section of state.schema) {
    const field = section.fields.find(item => item.key === key);
    if (field) return field;
  }
  return null;
}

function readControl(field, control) {
  if (field.type === 'checkbox') return control.checked;
  if (field.type === 'number') return control.value === '' ? '' : Number(control.value);
  if (field.type === 'string-list') {
    return control.value.split(',').map(item => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  }
  return control.value;
}

function displayValue(field, value) {
  if (field.type === 'string-list') return Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : '';
  if (field.type === 'checkbox') return !!value;
  return value ?? '';
}

function effectLabel(effect) {
  if (effect === 'reload') return 'Reload required';
  if (effect === 'next-launch') return 'Next launch';
  return '';
}

function createControl(field) {
  let control;
  if (field.type === 'select') {
    control = document.createElement('select');
    for (const [value, label] of field.options) {
      const option = document.createElement('option');
      option.value = value;
      option.textContent = label;
      control.append(option);
    }
  } else {
    control = document.createElement('input');
    control.type = field.type === 'checkbox' ? 'checkbox' : field.type === 'number' ? 'number' : 'text';
    if (field.min !== undefined) control.min = String(field.min);
    if (field.max !== undefined) control.max = String(field.max);
    if (field.step !== undefined) control.step = String(field.step);
    if (field.placeholder) control.placeholder = field.placeholder;
    if (field.type === 'shortcut') control.autocomplete = 'off';
  }
  control.id = `setting-${field.key}`;
  control.dataset.key = field.key;
  control.value = displayValue(field, state.values[field.key]);
  if (field.type === 'checkbox') control.checked = !!state.values[field.key];
  control.addEventListener('input', () => {
    state.values[field.key] = readControl(field, control);
    setMessage('');
    scheduleValidation();
  });
  return control;
}

function render() {
  nav.replaceChildren();
  sectionsHost.replaceChildren();

  for (const section of state.schema) {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = `#section-${section.id}`;
    link.textContent = section.title;
    nav.append(link);

    const fragment = template.content.cloneNode(true);
    const sectionElement = fragment.querySelector('section');
    sectionElement.id = `section-${section.id}`;
    fragment.querySelector('h2').textContent = section.title;
    fragment.querySelector('.section-heading p').textContent = section.description || '';
    const fieldsHost = fragment.querySelector('.fields');
    const resetSection = document.createElement('button');
    resetSection.type = 'button';
    resetSection.textContent = 'Reset section to defaults';
    resetSection.dataset.sectionReset = section.id;
    resetSection.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (state.saving) return;
      for (const field of section.fields) {
        if (Object.hasOwn(state.defaults, field.key)) state.values[field.key] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.defaults[field.key]));
      }
      syncControlsFromState();
      scheduleValidation();
      setMessage(`${section.title} reset to defaults. Save changes to apply.`);
    });
    fragment.querySelector('.section-heading').append(resetSection);

    for (const field of section.fields) {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'field';
      row.dataset.key = field.key;

      const label = document.createElement('label');
      label.className = 'field-label';
      label.htmlFor = `setting-${field.key}`;
      const text = document.createElement('span');
      text.textContent = field.label;
      label.append(text);
      const badgeText = effectLabel(field.effect);
      if (badgeText) {
        const badge = document.createElement('span');
        badge.className = 'effect-badge';
        badge.textContent = badgeText;
        label.append(badge);
      }

      const controlWrap = document.createElement('div');
      controlWrap.className = 'control-wrap';
      const control = createControl(field);
      controlWrap.append(control);
      if (field.suffix) {
        const suffix = document.createElement('span');
        suffix.className = 'suffix';
        suffix.textContent = field.suffix;
        controlWrap.append(suffix);
      }

      const error = document.createElement('div');
      error.className = 'field-error';
      error.id = `error-${field.key}`;
      error.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      control.setAttribute('aria-describedby', error.id);

      row.append(label, controlWrap, error);
      fieldsHost.append(row);
    }

    sectionsHost.append(fragment);
  }
  updateCurrentSection();
  updateUiState();
}

function updateCurrentSection() {
  const links = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('a'));
  const current = links.find(link => link.hash === window.location?.hash) || links[0];
  for (const link of links) {
    if (link === current) link.setAttribute('aria-current', 'location');
    else link.removeAttribute('aria-current');
  }
}

window.addEventListener('hashchange', updateCurrentSection);

function focusFirstInvalidField() {
  const control = form.querySelector('[aria-invalid="true"]:not(:disabled)');
  if (control) {
    control.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    control.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'instant' });
  } else {
    message.focus();
  }
}

function setMessage(text, kind = '') {
  message.textContent = text;
  message.className = `form-message ${kind}`.trim();
}

function renderErrors() {
  for (const row of document.querySelectorAll('.field')) {
    const key = row.dataset.key;
    const error = state.errors[key] || '';
    row.classList.toggle('invalid', !!error);
    const errorNode = row.querySelector('.field-error');
    if (errorNode) errorNode.textContent = error;
    const control = row.querySelector('[data-key]');
    if (control) control.setAttribute('aria-invalid', error ? 'true' : 'false');
  }
}

function updateUiState() {
  const dirty = isDirty();
  const invalid = Object.keys(state.errors).length > 0;
  dirtyBadge.hidden = !dirty;
  sectionsHost.setAttribute('aria-busy', String(state.validating || state.saving));
  saveButton.disabled = !dirty || invalid || state.saving || state.validating;
  resetButton.disabled = !dirty || state.saving;

  const reload = state.effects.filter(item => item.effect === 'reload').map(item => item.label);
  const nextLaunch = state.effects.filter(item => item.effect === 'next-launch').map(item => item.label);
  const parts = [];
  if (reload.length) parts.push(`Reload required: ${reload.join(', ')}`);
  if (nextLaunch.length) parts.push(`Applies next launch: ${nextLaunch.join(', ')}`);
  effectSummary.textContent = parts.join(' • ');
  for (const control of form.querySelectorAll('input, select, [data-section-reset]')) control.disabled = state.saving;
  document.getElementById('restore-previous').disabled = state.saving || !state.configuration.canRestore;
  document.getElementById('export-settings').disabled = state.saving;
  document.getElementById('import-settings').disabled = state.saving;
  renderErrors();
}

async function validate() {
  if (state.saving) return;
  const generation = ++state.validationGeneration;
  state.validating = true;
  updateUiState();
  try {
    const result = await window.appSettings.validate(collectPatch());
    if (generation !== state.validationGeneration || state.saving) return;
    state.errors = result.errors || {};
    state.effects = result.effects || [];
    if (state.errors._form) setMessage(state.errors._form, 'error');
  } catch (error) {
    if (generation !== state.validationGeneration || state.saving) return;
    state.errors = { _form: String(error?.message || error) };
    setMessage('Settings could not be validated.', 'error');
  } finally {
    if (generation === state.validationGeneration && !state.saving) {
      state.validating = false;
      updateUiState();
    }
  }
}

// Invalidate both in-flight responses and work still waiting in the debounce.
function invalidateValidation() {
  state.validationGeneration += 1;
  state.validating = false;
  if (state.validationTimer !== null) clearTimeout(state.validationTimer);
  state.validationTimer = null;
}

function scheduleValidation() {
  invalidateValidation();
  if (state.saving) return;
  state.validating = true;
  state.validationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    state.validationTimer = null;
    void validate();
  }, 120);
  updateUiState();
}

function syncControlsFromState() {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(state.values)) {
    const field = fieldForKey(key);
    const control = document.getElementById(`setting-${key}`);
    if (!field || !control) continue;
    if (field.type === 'checkbox') control.checked = !!value;
    else control.value = displayValue(field, value);
  }
}

function restoreBaseline() {
  if (state.saving) return;
  invalidateValidation();
  state.values = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.baseline));
  state.errors = {};
  state.effects = [];
  syncControlsFromState();
  setMessage('Changes reset.');
  updateUiState();
}

form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
  event.preventDefault();
  if (state.saving) return;
  state.saving = true;
  invalidateValidation();
  updateUiState();
  setMessage('Validating and saving…');
  let saveFailed = false;
  try {
    const result = await window.appSettings.save(collectPatch());
    state.errors = result.errors || {};
    state.effects = result.effects || [];
    if (!result.valid) {
      saveFailed = true;
      setMessage('Fix the highlighted settings before saving.', 'error');
      return;
    }

    state.values = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.values));
    state.baseline = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.values));
    showConfiguration(result.configuration);
    syncControlsFromState();
    const applyErrors = result.applyResult?.errors || [];
    const reload = state.effects.filter(item => item.effect === 'reload').map(item => item.label);
    const details = [];
    if (reload.length) details.push(`Reload required for: ${reload.join(', ')}.`);
    if (applyErrors.length) details.push(applyErrors.map(item => item.message).join(' '));
    setMessage(details.length ? `Settings saved. ${details.join(' ')}` : 'Settings saved and applied.', applyErrors.length ? 'error' : 'success');
  } catch (error) {
    saveFailed = true;
    setMessage(`Settings could not be saved: ${String(error?.message || error)}`, 'error');
  } finally {
    state.saving = false;
    updateUiState();
    if (saveFailed) focusFirstInvalidField();
  }
});

function showConfiguration(configuration = {}) {
  state.configuration = configuration;
  document.getElementById('config-path').textContent = configuration.configPath || 'Unavailable';
  document.getElementById('recovery-message').textContent = configuration.recovery?.message || '';
}

async function backupAction(action) {
  if (state.saving) return;
  if ((action === 'restorePrevious' || action === 'importSettings') && isDirty()
      && !window.confirm('Discard unsaved changes if this operation succeeds?')) return;
  state.saving = true;
  invalidateValidation();
  updateUiState();
  try {
    const result = await window.appSettings[action]();
    if (result.cancelled) return;
    if (result.valid === false) {
      setMessage(`Operation rejected: ${Object.values(result.errors || {}).join(' ')}`, 'error');
      return;
    }
    if (result.values) {
      state.values = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.values));
      if (action === 'restorePrevious') state.baseline = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result.values));
      state.errors = {};
      state.effects = result.effects || [];
      if (result.configuration) showConfiguration(result.configuration);
      syncControlsFromState();
    }
    const errors = result.applyResult?.errors || [];
    const effects = (result.effects || []).filter(item => item.effect !== 'immediate')
      .map(item => `${item.label}: ${effectLabel(item.effect)}`).join('; ');
    setMessage(action === 'importSettings' ? 'Imported settings staged for review. Save changes to apply.'
      : action === 'exportSettings' ? 'Saved settings exported.'
      : `Previous configuration restored. ${effects} ${errors.map(item => item.message).join(' ')}`,
      errors.length ? 'error' : 'success');
  } catch (error) {
    setMessage(`Operation failed: ${error.message || error}`, 'error');
  } finally {
    state.saving = false;
    updateUiState();
  }
}

document.getElementById('restore-previous').addEventListener('click', () => backupAction('restorePrevious'));
document.getElementById('export-settings').addEventListener('click', () => backupAction('exportSettings'));
document.getElementById('import-settings').addEventListener('click', () => backupAction('importSettings'));

resetButton.addEventListener('click', restoreBaseline);

window.addEventListener('beforeunload', event => {
  if (!isDirty()) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  event.returnValue = '';
});

(async function init() {
  try {
    const payload = await window.appSettings.get();
    state.schema = payload.schema || [];
    state.defaults = payload.defaults || {};
    showConfiguration(payload.configuration);
    state.values = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload.values || {}));
    state.baseline = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload.values || {}));
    document.title = `${payload.appLabel || 'Application'} Settings`;
    render();
    await validate();
  } catch (error) {
    setMessage(`Settings could not be loaded: ${String(error?.message || error)}`, 'error');
    saveButton.disabled = true;
    resetButton.disabled = true;
  }
})();

===== END FILE: renderer/settings.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/atomic-file.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { replaceFileFromTemporary } = require('../lib/atomic-file');

async function makeDirectory(t) {
  const directory = await fs.promises.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'atomic-file-test-'));
  t.after(() => fs.promises.rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  return directory;
}

test('Windows replacement swaps an existing destination and removes its backup', async t => {
  const directory = await makeDirectory(t);
  const destination = path.join(directory, 'export.txt');
  const temporary = path.join(directory, '.export.pending.tmp');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(destination, 'old', 'utf8');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(temporary, 'new', 'utf8');

  const result = await replaceFileFromTemporary(temporary, destination, { platform: 'win32' });

  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(destination, 'utf8'), 'new');
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(temporary), false);
  assert.equal(result.replacedExisting, true);
  assert.equal(result.cleanupError, null);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(directory), ['export.txt']);
});

test('Windows replacement restores the old destination when installing the temporary file fails', async t => {
  const directory = await makeDirectory(t);
  const destination = path.join(directory, 'export.txt');
  const temporary = path.join(directory, '.export.pending.tmp');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(destination, 'old', 'utf8');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(temporary, 'new', 'utf8');

  let failedInstall = false;
  const fsProxy = {
    promises: {
      ...fs.promises,
      rename: async (source, target) => {
        if (!failedInstall && source === temporary && target === destination) {
          failedInstall = true;
          const error = new Error('simulated sharing violation');
          error.code = 'EPERM';
          throw error;
        }
        return fs.promises.rename(source, target);
      },
    },
  };

  await assert.rejects(
    replaceFileFromTemporary(temporary, destination, { platform: 'win32', fs: fsProxy }),
    /simulated sharing violation/
  );
  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(destination, 'utf8'), 'old');
  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(temporary, 'utf8'), 'new');
  const leftovers = (await fs.promises.readdir(directory)).filter(name => name.includes('replace-backup'));
  assert.deepEqual(leftovers, []);
});

===== END FILE: test/atomic-file.test.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/canonical-conversation.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const os = require('os');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const {
  createCanonicalConversationStore,
  validateCanonicalCapture,
  logicalAnchorKey,
  intervalCoverage,
} = require('../lib/canonical-conversation');

function ref(key, extra = {}) {
  return { key, sourceKey: `message:${key}`, role: 'unknown', fingerprint: `${key}:fp`, textLength: 10, ...extra };
}
function rec(key, html, extra = {}) {
  return { ...ref(key, extra), html: html || `<article>${key}</article>` };
}

test('store merges overlapping windows into conversation order and keeps the richest copy', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-v2' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());
  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('a'), ref('b')], records: [rec('a'), rec('b')], scrollTop: 0, clientHeight: 500, range: 900, atTop: true });
  assert.equal(store.hasContinuity([ref('b'), ref('c')]), true);
  assert.equal(store.hasContinuity([ref('x')]), false);
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [ref('b'), ref('c'), ref('d')],
    records: [rec('b', '<article>b with a much longer tail</article>', { textLength: 30 }), rec('c'), rec('d')],
    scrollTop: 400, clientHeight: 500, range: 900, atBottom: true,
  });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });
  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(e => e.key), ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd']);
  assert.match(await fs.promises.readFile(manifest.entries[1].rawPath, 'utf8'), /longer tail/);
  const result = validateCanonicalCapture(manifest);
  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, false, result.failures.join('\n'));
});

test('a new turn seen before known turns is inserted ahead of them', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-v2' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());
  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('b'), ref('c')], records: [rec('b'), rec('c')], atTop: true, atBottom: true });
  store.endPass();
  store.beginPass('verify');
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('a'), ref('b')], records: [rec('a')], atTop: true, atBottom: true });
  store.endPass();
  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(e => e.key), ['a', 'b', 'c']);
  const result = validateCanonicalCapture(manifest);
  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, true);
  assert.match(result.failures.join('\n'), /verification pass still discovered 1/);
});

test('validation fails on continuity breaks and ordinal gaps, but pixel gaps are informational', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-v2' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());
  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('a', { ordinal: 1 })], records: [rec('a', null, { ordinal: 1 })], scrollTop: 0, clientHeight: 100, range: 5000, atTop: true });
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('c', { ordinal: 3 })], records: [rec('c', null, { ordinal: 3 })], scrollTop: 4900, clientHeight: 100, range: 5000, atBottom: true });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });
  const result = validateCanonicalCapture(store.buildManifest());
  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, true);
  const text = result.failures.join('\n');
  assert.match(text, /could not be linked/);
  assert.match(text, /1 turn position/);
});

test('logical anchors prefer source identity, then role plus ordinal', () => {
  assert.equal(logicalAnchorKey({ sourceKey: 'message:abc', role: 'assistant', ordinal: 7 }), 'source:message:abc');
  assert.equal(logicalAnchorKey({ role: 'assistant', ordinal: 7 }), 'ordinal:assistant:7');
  assert.equal(logicalAnchorKey({ role: 'assistant' }), '');
});

test('interval coverage reports internal gaps', () => {
  const result = intervalCoverage([[0, 300], [500, 1000]], 500, 500);
  assert.equal(result.gapPx, 200);
  assert.equal(result.coveragePct, 80);
});


test('store refreshes a learned logical identity when a richer observation arrives', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-identity' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [{ ...ref('stable'), sourceKey: '' }],
    records: [{ ...rec('stable', '<article>draft</article>', { textLength: 5 }), sourceKey: '' }],
    atTop: true,
  });
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [ref('stable', { sourceKey: 'message:learned' })],
    records: [rec('stable', '<article>draft with complete details</article>', {
      sourceKey: 'message:learned',
      textLength: 27,
    })],
    atBottom: true,
  });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 1);
  assert.equal(manifest.entries[0].sourceKey, 'message:learned');
  assert.equal(manifest.entries[0].logicalAnchor, 'source:message:learned');
});

test('a richer observation without a role preserves the learned role', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-role' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [ref('stable', { role: 'assistant' })],
    records: [rec('stable', '<article>draft</article>', { role: 'assistant', textLength: 5 })],
    atTop: true,
  });
  const richer = rec('stable', '<article>draft with complete details</article>', { textLength: 27 });
  delete richer.role;
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('stable')], records: [richer], atBottom: true });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });

  assert.equal(store.buildManifest().entries[0].role, 'assistant');
});

test('store preserves repeated identical content when stable keys differ', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-repeat' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  const first = rec('repeat-1', '<article>same text</article>', { fingerprint: 'same' });
  const second = rec('repeat-2', '<article>same text</article>', { fingerprint: 'same' });
  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [first, second],
    records: [first, second],
    atTop: true,
    atBottom: true,
  });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });

  assert.deepEqual(store.buildManifest().entries.map(entry => entry.key), ['repeat-1', 'repeat-2']);
});

test('store adopts streamed turn files and removes every unused staged observation', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-stream' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  async function stagedRecord(key, html, textLength) {
    const stagedPath = await store.createIncomingFile(key);
    await fs.promises.writeFile(stagedPath, html, 'utf8');
    return {
      ...rec(key, '', { textLength }),
      html: '',
      htmlLength: html.length,
      stagedPath,
    };
  }

  store.beginPass('capture');
  const first = await stagedRecord('streamed', '<article>first complete copy</article>', 19);
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('streamed')], records: [first], atTop: true });
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(first.stagedPath), false);

  const redundant = await stagedRecord('streamed', '<article>x</article>', 1);
  const unreferenced = await stagedRecord('not-mounted', '<article>unused</article>', 6);
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('streamed')], records: [redundant, unreferenced], atBottom: true });
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(redundant.stagedPath), false);
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(unreferenced.stagedPath), false);
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 1);
  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(manifest.entries[0].rawPath, 'utf8'), '<article>first complete copy</article>');
});

test('a failed staged replacement preserves the committed turn and removes temporary files', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-atomic' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  store.beginPass('capture');
  await store.ingestBatch({
    refs: [ref('stable')],
    records: [rec('stable', '<article>committed</article>', { textLength: 9 })],
    atTop: true,
  });

  const committed = store.entries.get('stable');
  const committedPath = committed.rawPath;
  const committedScore = committed.score;
  const stagedPath = await store.createIncomingFile('stable-richer');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(stagedPath, '<article>uncommitted richer replacement</article>', 'utf8');
  const richer = {
    ...rec('stable', '', { textLength: 99, fingerprint: 'replacement' }),
    html: '',
    htmlLength: 50,
    stagedPath,
  };

  const replace = store._replaceFileFromTemporary;
  store._replaceFileFromTemporary = async () => {
    const error = new Error('simulated replacement failure');
    error.code = 'EIO';
    throw error;
  };
  await assert.rejects(
    store.ingestBatch({ refs: [ref('stable')], records: [richer], atBottom: true }),
    /simulated replacement failure/
  );
  store._replaceFileFromTemporary = replace;

  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(committedPath, 'utf8'), '<article>committed</article>');
  assert.equal(committed.score, committedScore);
  assert.equal(committed.fingerprint, 'stable:fp');
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(stagedPath), false);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(store.incomingDirectory), []);
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(store.turnDirectory), [path.basename(committedPath)]);
});

test('staged-record cleanup never deletes a path outside the incoming directory', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-safe-cleanup' });
  const outsideDirectory = await fs.promises.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'canon-outside-'));
  const outsidePath = path.join(outsideDirectory, 'keep.txt');
  await fs.promises.writeFile(outsidePath, 'keep', 'utf8');
  t.after(async () => {
    await store.dispose();
    await fs.promises.rm(outsideDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  await store.discardStagedRecords([{ stagedPath: outsidePath }]);
  assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(outsidePath, 'utf8'), 'keep');
});

test('expected total uses the dominant virtualizer observation rather than one outlier', async t => {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'canon-total' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  store.beginPass('capture');
  const rows = [
    rec('one', null, { ordinal: 1, setSize: 999 }),
    rec('two', null, { ordinal: 2, setSize: 3 }),
    rec('three', null, { ordinal: 3, setSize: 3 }),
  ];
  await store.ingestBatch({ refs: rows, records: rows, atTop: true, atBottom: true });
  store.endPass({ terminalStableSamples: 3 });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.expectedTotal, 3);
  assert.equal(manifest.ordinalsComplete, true);
});

===== BEGIN FILE: test/conversation-capture.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const os = require('os');
const fs = require('fs');

const { createCanonicalConversationStore, validateCanonicalCapture } = require('../lib/canonical-conversation');
const { captureConversation } = require('../lib/conversation-capture');

// Simulated virtualizer: turns with heights, a viewport, overscan, optional
// re-measurement (heights change after first mount) and optional ordinals.
function makePage({ heights, viewport = 800, overscan = 200, remeasure = null, ordinals = false, lazyTop = 0 }) {
  let turns = heights.map((h, i) => ({ key: `t${i + 1}`, h, measured: false, i }));
  const hidden = turns.splice(0, lazyTop); // older history loaded at the top
  let scrollTop = 0;
  const sent = new Set();
  const layout = () => { let y = 0; return turns.map(t => { const r = { t, y }; y += t.h; return r; }); };
  const total = () => turns.reduce((s, t) => s + t.h, 0);
  const range = () => Math.max(0, total() - viewport);
  let topVisits = 0;
  return {
    sentCount: () => sent.size,
    async callBatch(opts) {
      for (const k of opts.resend || []) sent.delete(k);
      const r = range();
      const target = opts.target === 'end' ? r : Math.max(0, Math.min(r, Number(opts.target) || 0));
      scrollTop = target;
      if (scrollTop === 0 && hidden.length && ++topVisits > 1) { turns = hidden.splice(0).concat(turns); }
      const mounted = layout().filter(({ t, y }) => y + t.h > scrollTop - overscan && y < scrollTop + viewport + overscan);
      if (remeasure) {
        for (const { t } of mounted) if (!t.measured) { t.measured = true; t.h = remeasure(t); }
        // keep the first mounted turn anchored (scroll anchoring)
      }
      const refs = mounted.map(({ t, y }) => ({
        key: t.key, sourceKey: `message:${t.key}`, role: 'unknown', fingerprint: t.key,
        textLength: 20, y, ...(ordinals ? { ordinal: t.i + 1, setSize: heights.length } : {}),
      }));
      const records = opts.observeOnly ? [] : refs.filter(x => !sent.has(x.key)).map(x => (sent.add(x.key), { ...x, html: `<p>${x.key}</p>` }));
      const nr = range();
      scrollTop = Math.min(scrollTop, nr);
      return { ok: true, refs, records, scrollTop, clientHeight: viewport, range: nr, atTop: scrollTop <= 2, atBottom: nr <= 2 || scrollTop >= nr - 2 };
    },
  };
}

async function run(page, config = {}) {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'capture-test' });
  try {
    await captureConversation({ store, callBatch: page.callBatch, config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1, ...config } });
    const manifest = store.buildManifest();
    return { manifest, completeness: validateCanonicalCapture(manifest) };
  } finally {
    await store.dispose();
  }
}

test('captures every turn of a long conversation in order', async () => {
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => 80 + ((i * 137) % 900));
  const { manifest, completeness } = await run(makePage({ heights }));
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 300);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(e => e.key), heights.map((_, i) => `t${i + 1}`));
  assert.equal(completeness.lowConfidence, false, completeness.failures.join('\n'));
});

test('survives turns taller than the viewport and late re-measurement', async () => {
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => (i % 7 === 0 ? 5000 : 120));
  const page = makePage({ heights, overscan: 0, remeasure: t => t.h * (t.i % 3 === 0 ? 3 : 1) });
  const { manifest, completeness } = await run(page);
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 60);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(e => e.key), heights.map((_, i) => `t${i + 1}`));
  assert.equal(completeness.lowConfidence, false, completeness.failures.join('\n'));
});

test('skips verification when ordinals prove completeness', async () => {
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 40 }, () => 300);
  const { manifest, completeness } = await run(makePage({ heights, ordinals: true }));
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 40);
  assert.equal(manifest.passes.length, 1);
  assert.equal(completeness.lowConfidence, false, completeness.failures.join('\n'));
});

test('picks up history that loads when the top is reached', async () => {
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 50 }, () => 250);
  const { manifest, completeness } = await run(makePage({ heights, lazyTop: 10 }));
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 50);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(e => e.key), heights.map((_, i) => `t${i + 1}`));
  assert.equal(completeness.lowConfidence, false, completeness.failures.join('\n'));
});


// Wrap makePage() so it behaves like the streaming renderer: records carry a
// recordToken and htmlLength, HTML is read in chunks, and the next batch is
// refused while any token is still pending.
function makeStreamingPage(pageOptions, htmlFor) {
  const page = makePage(pageOptions);
  const pending = new Map();
  const stats = { chunkReads: 0, charsRead: 0, released: 0 };
  let serial = 0;
  return {
    pending,
    stats,
    async callBatch(opts) {
      if (pending.size) return { ok: false, reason: 'pending-canonical-records-not-released' };
      const batch = await page.callBatch(opts);
      if (!opts.streamRecords || opts.observeOnly) return batch;
      batch.records = batch.records.map(record => {
        const html = htmlFor(record.key);
        const token = `record-${++serial}`;
        pending.set(token, html);
        const metadata = { ...record };
        delete metadata.html;
        return { ...metadata, recordToken: token, htmlLength: html.length };
      });
      return batch;
    },
    async readRecordChunk({ recordToken, offset, maxChars }) {
      const html = pending.get(recordToken);
      if (typeof html !== 'string') return { ok: false, reason: 'missing-token' };
      let end = Math.min(html.length, offset + maxChars);
      if (end < html.length && end > offset) {
        const last = html.charCodeAt(end - 1);
        if (last >= 0xD800 && last <= 0xDBFF) end -= 1;
      }
      stats.chunkReads += 1;
      stats.charsRead += end - offset;
      return { ok: true, chunk: html.slice(offset, end), nextOffset: end, totalLength: html.length };
    },
    async releaseRecords({ recordTokens }) {
      let released = 0;
      for (const token of recordTokens) {
        if (pending.delete(token)) released += 1;
      }
      stats.released += released;
      return { ok: true, released };
    },
  };
}

test('streams large turns in bounded chunks without splitting surrogate pairs', async t => {
  // The emoji straddles the 16384-character chunk boundary.
  const expectedHtml = `<article>${'x'.repeat(16374)}\u{1F600}${'y'.repeat(20000)}</article>`;
  const page = makeStreamingPage({ heights: [300, 300], viewport: 800, ordinals: true }, () => expectedHtml);
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'capture-stream' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  await captureConversation({
    store,
    callBatch: opts => page.callBatch(opts),
    readRecordChunk: opts => page.readRecordChunk(opts),
    releaseRecords: opts => page.releaseRecords(opts),
    config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1, exportCaptureIpcChunkBytes: 16384 },
  });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 2);
  assert.equal(page.pending.size, 0);
  assert.ok(page.stats.chunkReads >= 6);
  for (const entry of manifest.entries) {
    assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(entry.rawPath, 'utf8'), expectedHtml);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(store.incomingDirectory), []);
});

test('rejected streamed windows are released without transferring their HTML', async t => {
  // Placeholder rows shrink after mounting, forcing at least one continuity
  // backtrack. Streamed records from that rejected window must not be read.
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 80 }, () => 900);
  const page = makeStreamingPage(
    { heights, overscan: 0, remeasure: turn => (turn.i % 2 ? 60 : 900) },
    key => `<p>${key}</p>`
  );
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'capture-reject' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  await captureConversation({
    store,
    callBatch: opts => page.callBatch(opts),
    readRecordChunk: opts => page.readRecordChunk(opts),
    releaseRecords: opts => page.releaseRecords(opts),
    config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1, exportCaptureStepFraction: 0.9 },
  });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 80);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(entry => entry.key), heights.map((_, index) => `t${index + 1}`));
  assert.ok(manifest.passes.some(pass => pass.backtracks > 0), 'scenario must exercise rejection');
  const keptCharacters = manifest.entries.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.htmlLength, 0);
  assert.equal(page.stats.charsRead, keptCharacters);
  assert.equal(page.pending.size, 0);
  assert.equal(validateCanonicalCapture(manifest).lowConfidence, false);
});

test('rejected pre-staged windows are cleaned up before retrying', async t => {
  const heights = Array.from({ length: 80 }, (_, index) => (index % 5 === 0 ? 40 : 600));
  const page = makePage({ heights, overscan: 0 });
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'capture-staged' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  const callBatch = async opts => {
    const batch = await page.callBatch(opts);
    const records = [];
    for (const record of batch.records) {
      const stagedPath = await store.createIncomingFile(record.key);
      await fs.promises.writeFile(stagedPath, record.html, 'utf8');
      const { html, ...metadata } = record;
      records.push({ ...metadata, htmlLength: html.length, stagedPath });
    }
    return { ...batch, records };
  };

  await captureConversation({
    store,
    callBatch,
    config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1, exportCaptureStepFraction: 0.9 },
  });

  const manifest = store.buildManifest();
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 80);
  assert.deepEqual(manifest.entries.map(entry => entry.key), heights.map((_, index) => `t${index + 1}`));
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(store.incomingDirectory), []);
});

test('a release failure does not hide the primary streaming error', async t => {
  const page = makeStreamingPage({ heights: [300], viewport: 800 }, () => '<p>x</p>');
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'capture-fail' });
  t.after(() => store.dispose());

  await assert.rejects(
    captureConversation({
      store,
      callBatch: opts => page.callBatch(opts),
      readRecordChunk: async () => ({ ok: false, reason: 'renderer-chunk-broken' }),
      releaseRecords: async () => ({ ok: false, reason: 'release-broken' }),
      config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1 },
    }),
    /renderer-chunk-broken/
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await fs.promises.readdir(store.incomingDirectory), []);
});

===== BEGIN FILE: test/dom-adapter.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const {
  chatDom,
  createHarness,
  deepClone,
  defaultAgentConfig,
  loadFixture,
} = require('../test-support/dom-agent-harness');

const SHARED_CONTRACT = {
  version: 1,
  thresholds: { read: 55, interact: 70, destructive: 85 },
  negativeRegionSelectors: [
    'nav', 'header', 'aside', '[role="navigation"]', '[role="toolbar"]',
    '[role="dialog"]', '[role="search"]', '[role="menu"]',
  ],
  capabilities: {
    chatRoot: {
      selectors: ['[data-chat-root]'],
      semanticSelectors: ['[role="feed"]', '[role="log"]', 'main', '[role="main"]'],
      repeatedDescendants: ['article'],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
    messageRow: {
      selectors: ['article'],
      semanticSelectors: ['article', '[role="article"]', '[aria-posinset]'],
      stableAttributeHints: ['message', 'turn', 'response', 'request'],
    },
    composer: {
      selectors: ['textarea', '[contenteditable="true"]', 'div[role="textbox"]'],
      semanticSelectors: ['textarea', '[contenteditable="true"]', '[role="textbox"]'],
      stableAttributeHints: ['composer'],
    },
    disclosureControl: {
      selectors: ['button[aria-expanded]', '[role="button"][aria-expanded]', 'summary'],
      semanticSelectors: ['button[aria-expanded]', '[role="button"][aria-expanded]', 'summary'],
      stableAttributeHints: ['expand', 'collapse', 'disclosure'],
    },
    virtualizer: { selectors: [], stableAttributeHints: [] },
  },
};

function sharedAgentConfig(contract = SHARED_CONTRACT) {
  const cloned = deepClone(contract);
  const capabilities = cloned.capabilities || {};
  return {
    chatRootSelectors: (capabilities.chatRoot && capabilities.chatRoot.selectors) || [],
    virtualizerSelectors: (capabilities.virtualizer && capabilities.virtualizer.selectors) || [],
    chatInputSelectors: (capabilities.composer && capabilities.composer.selectors) || [],
    scoreRules: [],
    collectionSelectors: ['article'],
    collectionKeyAttributes: ['data-message-id', 'data-testid', 'id', 'aria-label'],
    collectionRowSelectors: ['article'],
    collectionExcludeSelectors: [],
    domAdapterContract: cloned,
  };
}

function useHarness(t, fixture, options = {}) {
  const harness = createHarness(fixture, options);
  t.after(() => harness.dispose());
  return harness;
}

function negativeRegionFixture(region) {
  const candidateRegion = deepClone(region);
  candidateRegion.children = [
    {
      tag: 'textarea',
      attrs: { id: 'composer-decoy', 'aria-label': 'Not the chat composer' },
    },
  ];
  return {
    body: {
      children: [
        {
          tag: 'div',
          attrs: { id: 'app-shell' },
          children: [
            {
              tag: 'div',
              attrs: { id: 'conversation', role: 'feed', 'data-chat-root': 'candidate' },
              children: [
                { tag: 'article', attrs: { 'aria-posinset': '1', 'data-message-id': 'm1' }, text: 'One' },
                { tag: 'article', attrs: { 'aria-posinset': '2', 'data-message-id': 'm2' }, text: 'Two' },
              ],
            },
            candidateRegion,
          ],
        },
      ],
    },
  };
}

test('shared contract scores two plausible roots and chooses the stronger conversation', t => {
  const { api } = useHarness(t, 'two-roots.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });

  const hit = api.findCapability('chatRoot');

  assert.equal(hit.ok, true);
  assert.equal(hit.element.id, 'conversation-feed');
  assert.ok(hit.confidence >= SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.read);
  assert.match(hit.evidence.join('\n'), /5 repeated message descendants/);

  const located = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false });
  assert.equal(located.ok, true);
  assert.equal(located.confidence, hit.confidence);
});

test('all root consumers report the capability scorer winner', async t => {
  const contract = deepClone(SHARED_CONTRACT);
  contract.thresholds.destructive = 70;
  const { api, document } = useHarness(t, 'two-roots.json', {
    config: sharedAgentConfig(contract),
  });

  const authoritative = api.findCapability('chatRoot');
  const compatibility = api.pickBest();
  const located = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false });
  const selected = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false, selectContent: true });
  const exported = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false, markForExport: true });
  const find = api.enableFindContentVisibility();
  const health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  const composer = await api.waitForChatInputReady({ timeoutMs: 5 });

  assert.equal(authoritative.element.id, 'conversation-feed');
  assert.equal(compatibility.el, authoritative.element);
  assert.equal(compatibility.score, authoritative.confidence);
  assert.equal(document.querySelector('[data-pdf-export-target="1"]').id, 'conversation-feed');
  assert.equal(document.querySelector('[data-app-find-root="1"]').id, 'conversation-feed');
  assert.equal(composer.ready, true);
  assert.deepEqual(
    new Set([
      compatibility.rootIdentity,
      located.rootIdentity,
      selected.rootIdentity,
      exported.rootIdentity,
      find.rootIdentity,
      health.rootIdentity,
      health.selectedRoot,
      composer.rootIdentity,
    ]),
    new Set(['div#conversation-feed'])
  );
});

test('provider adapter selects its expected root from a realistic provider fixture', t => {
  const fixture = loadFixture('provider-root.json');
  const { api } = useHarness(t, fixture, { config: defaultAgentConfig() });

  const hit = api.findCapability('chatRoot');

  assert.equal(hit.ok, true);
  assert.equal(hit.element.id, fixture.expectedRootId);
  assert.ok(hit.confidence >= chatDom.DOM_ADAPTER_CONTRACT.thresholds.read);
  assert.match(hit.evidence.join('\n'), /repeated message descendants/);
});

test('a search textbox and chat composer can coexist without selecting search', t => {
  const { api } = useHarness(t, 'composer-and-search.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });
  const root = api.findCapability('chatRoot');

  const composer = api.findCapability('composer', { root: root.element, verifyBehavior: true });

  assert.equal(composer.ok, true);
  assert.equal(composer.element.id, 'composer-editor');
  assert.ok(composer.confidence >= SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.interact);
  assert.doesNotMatch(composer.warnings.join('\n'), /negative region/);
});

test('composer tracking moves markers when SPA navigation replaces the composer', async t => {
  const harness = useHarness(t, 'composer-tracking.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });
  const { api, document, fixture } = harness;

  const started = api.startComposerTracking({ throttleMs: 1 });
  assert.equal(started.ok, true);
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('route-a-form').getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), '1');
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('route-a-composer').getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), '1');

  const oldRoute = document.getElementById('route-a-composer');
  const appShell = document.getElementById('app-shell');
  oldRoute.remove();
  appShell.appendChild(harness.createElement(fixture.replacement));
  await harness.settle(10);

  assert.equal(document.getElementById('composer-b').isConnected, true);
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('route-b-form').getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), '1');
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('route-b-composer').getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), '1');
  assert.equal(oldRoute.getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), null);
});

test('typing mutations keep the composer fast path and avoid repeated document rescans', async t => {
  const harness = useHarness(t, 'composer-tracking.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });
  const { api, document } = harness;

  const started = api.startComposerTracking({ throttleMs: 1 });
  assert.equal(started.ok, true);
  document.resetQuerySelectorAllCalls();

  const editor = document.getElementById('composer-a');
  editor.textContent = 'Typing should not rediscover the entire document';
  await harness.settle(10);

  assert.equal(editor.isConnected, true);
  assert.equal(document.querySelectorAllCalls, 0, 'connected-editor mutations must not trigger capability rescans');
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('route-a-form').getAttribute('data-expanded-composer'), '1');
});

test('low-confidence root candidates are rejected before read or export operations', t => {
  const { api, document } = useHarness(t, 'low-confidence.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });

  const candidate = api.findCapability('chatRoot');
  assert.equal(candidate.ok, true);
  assert.ok(candidate.confidence < SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.read);

  const readAttempt = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false });
  assert.deepEqual(
    {
      ok: readAttempt.ok,
      reason: readAttempt.reason,
      operationLevel: readAttempt.operationLevel,
      requiredConfidence: readAttempt.requiredConfidence,
    },
    {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'low-confidence-chat-root',
      operationLevel: 'read',
      requiredConfidence: SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.read,
    }
  );

  const exportAttempt = api.locateChatRoot({ includeHtml: false, markForExport: true });
  assert.equal(exportAttempt.ok, false);
  assert.equal(exportAttempt.operationLevel, 'destructive');
  assert.equal(exportAttempt.requiredConfidence, SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.destructive);
  assert.equal(document.querySelector('[data-pdf-export-target="1"]'), null);

  const health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.status, 'Low confidence');
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.confidenceThreshold, SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.read);
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.requiredLevel, 'read');
});

test('navigation, dialogs, menus, and toolbars suppress composer candidates', async t => {
  const fixture = loadFixture('negative-regions.json');

  for (const fixtureCase of fixture.cases) {
    await t.test(fixtureCase.name, async t => {
      const { api } = useHarness(t, negativeRegionFixture(fixtureCase.region), { config: sharedAgentConfig() });
      const root = api.findCapability('chatRoot');
      const composer = api.findCapability('composer', { root: root.element, verifyBehavior: true });

      assert.equal(composer.ok, true);
      assert.ok(
        composer.confidence < SHARED_CONTRACT.thresholds.read,
        `${fixtureCase.name} candidate unexpectedly scored ${composer.confidence}`
      );
      assert.match(composer.warnings.join('\n'), /inside negative region/);

      const ready = await api.waitForChatInputReady({ timeoutMs: 2 });
      assert.deepEqual(
        { ok: ready.ok, ready: ready.ready, timeout: ready.timeout },
        { ok: true, ready: false, timeout: true }
      );
    });
  }
});

test('semantic-only chat root and composer fallbacks survive app fingerprint loss', t => {
  const semanticContract = deepClone(SHARED_CONTRACT);
  semanticContract.capabilities.chatRoot.selectors = [];
  semanticContract.capabilities.composer.selectors = [];
  const config = sharedAgentConfig(semanticContract);
  config.chatInputSelectors = ['textarea', '[contenteditable="true"]', '[role="textbox"]'];
  const { api } = useHarness(t, 'semantic-fallback.json', { config });

  const root = api.findCapability('chatRoot');
  assert.equal(root.ok, true);
  assert.equal(root.element.getAttribute('role'), 'log');
  assert.ok(root.confidence >= semanticContract.thresholds.read);
  assert.equal(root.tier, 'semantic-structural');
  assert.doesNotMatch(root.evidence.join('\n'), /configured selector evidence/);

  const health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.status, 'Fallback');
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.selectedTier, 'semantic-structural');

  const composer = api.findCapability('composer', { root: root.element, verifyBehavior: true });
  assert.equal(composer.ok, true);
  assert.equal(composer.element.getAttribute('aria-label'), 'Send a message');
  assert.ok(composer.confidence >= semanticContract.thresholds.interact);
  assert.doesNotMatch(composer.evidence.join('\n'), /configured selector evidence/);
});

test('one element discovered through duplicate selector tiers is scored once', t => {
  const duplicateContract = deepClone(SHARED_CONTRACT);
  duplicateContract.negativeRegionSelectors = [];
  duplicateContract.capabilities.chatRoot = {
    selectors: ['main', '#chat-root', '[role="main"]', 'main'],
    semanticSelectors: ['main', '[role="main"]', 'main'],
    repeatedDescendants: ['article', '[aria-posinset]'],
    stableAttributeHints: ['chat-root'],
  };
  const harness = useHarness(t, 'duplicate-tiers.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig(duplicateContract) });
  const duplicateHit = harness.api.findCapability('chatRoot');

  const simpleContract = deepClone(duplicateContract);
  simpleContract.capabilities.chatRoot.selectors = ['main'];
  simpleContract.capabilities.chatRoot.semanticSelectors = ['main'];
  simpleContract.capabilities.chatRoot.repeatedDescendants = ['article'];
  harness.api.init(sharedAgentConfig(simpleContract));
  const simpleHit = harness.api.findCapability('chatRoot');

  assert.equal(duplicateHit.element, simpleHit.element);
  assert.equal(duplicateHit.confidence, simpleHit.confidence);
  assert.equal(duplicateHit.evidence.filter(item => item === 'configured selector evidence').length, 1);
  assert.equal(new Set(duplicateHit.evidence).size, duplicateHit.evidence.length);
});

test('selector health remains structured when no root or composer is found', t => {
  const { api } = useHarness(t, 'empty-shell.json', { config: sharedAgentConfig() });

  const health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();

  assert.equal(health.ok, true);
  assert.equal(health.selectedRoot, null);
  assert.equal(health.selectedRootConfidence, 0);
  assert.equal(health.activeFallbackTier, 'none');
  assert.deepEqual(
    {
      rootFound: health.capabilities.chatRoot.found,
      rootConfidence: health.capabilities.chatRoot.confidence,
      rootWarnings: Array.from(health.capabilities.chatRoot.warnings),
      composerFound: health.capabilities.composer.found,
      composerConfidence: health.capabilities.composer.confidence,
      composerWarnings: Array.from(health.capabilities.composer.warnings),
    },
    {
      rootFound: false,
      rootConfidence: 0,
      rootWarnings: ['no candidates'],
      composerFound: false,
      composerConfidence: 0,
      composerWarnings: ['no candidates'],
    }
  );
  assert.equal(health.messageRowCount, 0);
  assert.equal(health.exportCompleteness.readReady, false);
  assert.equal(health.exportCompleteness.destructiveReady, false);
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.chatRoot.status, 'Missing');
  assert.equal(health.capabilities.composer.status, 'Missing');
});
