===== BEGIN FILE: test/electron/integration.js =====
'use strict';

const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('fs');
const http = require('http');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain } = require('electron');

const { createRendererAgentInstaller } = require('../../lib/renderer-agent-installer');
const { injectCSSOnLoad } = require('../../app-specific/layout-css');
const { createIPC } = require('../../lib/ipc');
const { createSettingsWindow } = require('../../lib/settings-window');
const { ExportJob, ExportCancelledError } = require('../../lib/export-job');
const { bindExportJobToWindow } = require('../../lib/window-job-lifecycle');
const { createCanonicalConversationStore } = require('../../lib/canonical-conversation');
const { captureConversation } = require('../../lib/conversation-capture');
const { callRendererMethodInFrame } = require('../../lib/renderer-api');
const { replaceFileFromTemporary } = require('../../lib/atomic-file');
const appConfig = require('../../app-specific/app.config');
const appSlug = appConfig.appSlug;
const appLabel = appConfig.appLabel;
const hostApiName = `${appSlug}Host`;

const repoRoot = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
const windows = new Set();

function delay(ms) {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function waitFor(check, options = {}) {
  const timeoutMs = Number(options.timeoutMs || 8000);
  const intervalMs = Number(options.intervalMs || 25);
  const started = Date.now();
  let lastError = null;
  while (Date.now() - started < timeoutMs) {
    try {
      const value = await check();
      if (value) return value;
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
    }
    await delay(intervalMs);
  }
  if (lastError) throw lastError;
  throw new Error(options.message || `Condition was not met within ${timeoutMs}ms.`);
}

function trackWindow(win) {
  windows.add(win);
  win.once('closed', () => windows.delete(win));
  return win;
}

function fixtureDocument(url) {
  const route = url.pathname;
  const revision = url.searchParams.get('rev') || '1';
  if (route === '/frame') {
    return `<!doctype html><html><body data-frame-revision="${revision}">
      <div id="frame-value">frame-${revision}</div>
      <script>
        window.__rpcFixture = {
          echo(value) { return { ok: true, value, revision: ${JSON.stringify(revision)} }; },
          slow(delayMs) { return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(() => resolve({ ok: true, revision: ${JSON.stringify(revision)} }), delayMs)); }
        };
      </script>
    </body></html>`;
  }

  return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head>
    <body data-page="${route}" data-revision="${revision}">
      <main id="fixture">fixture-${revision}</main>
      <section id="conversation"><article data-message-id="one">one</article></section>
      ${route === '/with-frame' ? `<iframe id="same-origin-frame" src="/frame?rev=${revision}"></iframe>` : ''}
      <script>
        window.captureCancelled = false;
        window.fixtureCaptureBatch = async function(payload) {
          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 80));
          if (window.captureCancelled) return { ok: false, cancelled: true, reason: 'cancelled' };
          return {
            ok: true,
            refs: [{ key: 'one', fingerprint: 'one:fp', ordinal: 1, setSize: 1 }],
            records: payload && payload.observeOnly ? [] : [{
              key: 'one', fingerprint: 'one:fp', ordinal: 1, setSize: 1,
              html: '<article>one</article>', htmlLength: 22, textLength: 3
            }],
            atTop: true,
            atBottom: true,
            range: 0,
            scrollTop: 0,
            clientHeight: 600
          };
        };
      </script>
    </body></html>`;
}

async function startFixtureServer() {
  const server = http.createServer((request, response) => {
    const url = new URL(request.url, 'http://127.0.0.1');
    const body = fixtureDocument(url);
    response.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    });
    response.end(body);
  });
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once('error', reject);
    server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve);
  });
  const address = server.address();
  return {
    origin: `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`,
    close: () => new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve)),
  };
}

async function createFixtureWindow(installer, url, webPreferences = {}) {
  const win = trackWindow(new BrowserWindow({
    show: false,
    width: 800,
    height: 600,
    webPreferences: {
      nodeIntegration: false,
      contextIsolation: true,
      sandbox: false,
      ...webPreferences,
    },
  }));
  if (installer) installer.install(win.webContents);
  await win.loadURL(url);
  return win;
}

async function destroyAllWindows() {
  for (const win of [...windows]) {
    try {
      if (!win.isDestroyed()) win.destroy();
    } catch {}
  }
  windows.clear();
  await delay(50);
}

async function runCase(name, testFunction) {
  const started = Date.now();
  try {
    await Promise.race([
      testFunction(),
      new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out: ${name}`)), 20000)),
    ]);
    console.log(`ok - ${name} (${Date.now() - started}ms)`);
    return { name, ok: true, elapsedMs: Date.now() - started };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`not ok - ${name}`);
    console.error(error?.stack || error);
    return { name, ok: false, elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, error };
  } finally {
    await destroyAllWindows();
  }
}

async function main() {
  const userData = await fs.promises.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'copilot-electron-integration-'));
  app.setPath('userData', userData);
  await app.whenReady();

  const fixture = await startFixtureServer();
  const configuredGlobal = '__fixtureRenderer';
  const configuredVersion = 73;
  const installer = createRendererAgentInstaller({
    fs,
    agentPath: path.join(repoRoot, 'renderer', 'agent.js'),
    rendererApiGlobal: configuredGlobal,
    rendererAgentVersion: configuredVersion,
    agentConfig: {
      chatRootSelectors: ['#conversation'],
      collectionSelectors: ['#conversation'],
      collectionRowSelectors: ['article'],
      collectionKeyAttributes: ['data-message-id'],
      collectionExcludeSelectors: [],
      junkSelectors: [],
      preserveSelectors: ['article'],
      virtualizerSelectors: [],
      scoreRules: {},
      chatInputSelectors: [],
      reasoningExpandSelectors: [],
    },
  });

  const cases = [];

  cases.push(await runCase('renderer agent survives full navigation with configured global and version', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(installer, `${fixture.origin}/page?rev=1`);
    const first = await waitFor(() => win.webContents.executeJavaScript(
      `window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}] && ({ version: window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}].__version, page: document.body.dataset.revision })`,
      true
    ));
    assert.deepEqual(first, { version: configuredVersion, page: '1' });

    await win.loadURL(`${fixture.origin}/page?rev=2`);
    const second = await waitFor(() => win.webContents.executeJavaScript(
      `window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}] && ({ version: window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}].__version, page: document.body.dataset.revision })`,
      true
    ));
    assert.deepEqual(second, { version: configuredVersion, page: '2' });
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('renderer agent is reinjected when a same-origin frame navigates', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(installer, `${fixture.origin}/with-frame?rev=1`);
    const getFrame = async revision => waitFor(async () => {
      const frames = win.webContents.mainFrame.framesInSubtree || [];
      const frame = frames.find(candidate => candidate !== win.webContents.mainFrame && candidate.url.includes(`/frame?rev=${revision}`));
      if (!frame) return null;
      const value = await frame.executeJavaScript(
        `window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}] && ({ version: window[${JSON.stringify(configuredGlobal)}].__version, revision: document.body.dataset.frameRevision })`,
        true
      );
      return value ? { frame, value } : null;
    });

    const first = await getFrame('1');
    assert.deepEqual(first.value, { version: configuredVersion, revision: '1' });
    await win.webContents.executeJavaScript("document.getElementById('same-origin-frame').src = '/frame?rev=2'", true);
    const second = await getFrame('2');
    assert.deepEqual(second.value, { version: configuredVersion, revision: '2' });
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('preloads remain available with context isolation and sandboxing', async () => {
    const IPC = createIPC(appSlug);
    let ping = null;
    let selectionMessage = null;
    const onRendererSettings = event => { event.returnValue = { directOpenBehavior: 'shift-click' }; };
    const onPing = (_event, value) => { ping = value; };
    const onSelection = (_event, value) => { selectionMessage = value; };
    ipcMain.on(IPC.RENDERER_SETTINGS, onRendererSettings);
    ipcMain.on(IPC.PRELOAD_PING, onPing);
    ipcMain.on(IPC.SEND_SELECTION, onSelection);
    try {
      const win = await createFixtureWindow(null, `${fixture.origin}/preload`, {
        preload: path.join(repoRoot, 'app-specific', 'preload.js'),
        contextIsolation: true,
        sandbox: false,
      });
      const availability = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`({
        host: typeof window[${JSON.stringify(hostApiName)}],
        sendSelection: typeof window[${JSON.stringify(hostApiName)}]?.sendSelection,
        rendererRequire: typeof window.require,
        rendererProcess: typeof window.process
      })`, true);
      assert.deepEqual(availability, {
        host: 'object',
        sendSelection: 'function',
        rendererRequire: 'undefined',
        rendererProcess: 'undefined',
      });
      await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`window[${JSON.stringify(hostApiName)}].sendSelection({ mode: 'quote', autoSubmit: true })`, true);
      await waitFor(() => ping && selectionMessage);
      assert.equal(selectionMessage.mode, 'quote');
      assert.equal(selectionMessage.autoSubmit, true);
    } finally {
      ipcMain.removeListener(IPC.RENDERER_SETTINGS, onRendererSettings);
      ipcMain.removeListener(IPC.PRELOAD_PING, onPing);
      ipcMain.removeListener(IPC.SEND_SELECTION, onSelection);
    }
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('layout CSS is inserted, replaced, and restored after reload', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(null, `${fixture.origin}/css`);
    const holder = { key: null, css: '', __wired: false };
    injectCSSOnLoad(win, '#fixture { color: rgb(255, 0, 0) !important; }', holder);
    await waitFor(async () => (await win.webContents.executeJavaScript("getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('fixture')).color", true)) === 'rgb(255, 0, 0)');
    const firstKey = holder.key;
    assert.ok(firstKey);

    injectCSSOnLoad(win, '#fixture { background-color: rgb(0, 0, 255) !important; }', holder);
    await waitFor(async () => {
      const styles = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`(() => {
        const value = getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('fixture'));
        return { color: value.color, background: value.backgroundColor };
      })()`, true);
      return styles.background === 'rgb(0, 0, 255)' && styles.color !== 'rgb(255, 0, 0)' && styles;
    });
    assert.notEqual(holder.key, firstKey);

    await win.reload();
    const afterReload = await waitFor(async () => {
      const styles = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`(() => {
        const value = getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('fixture'));
        return { color: value.color, background: value.backgroundColor };
      })()`, true);
      return styles.background === 'rgb(0, 0, 255)' && styles;
    });
    assert.notEqual(afterReload.color, 'rgb(255, 0, 0)');
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('settings IPC works through the sandboxed settings preload', async () => {
    let config = { ...appConfig.defaultAppConfig };
    const IPC = createIPC(appSlug);
    const manager = createSettingsWindow({
      BrowserWindow,
      ipcMain,
      path,
      dirname: repoRoot,
      IPC,
      appLabel: `${appLabel} Fixture`,
      getAppConfig: () => config,
      updateAppConfig: patch => (config = { ...config, ...patch }),
      validateRuntimeSettings: async () => ({ ok: true }),
      applyRuntimeSettings: async () => ({ ok: true, applied: true }),
      getAppIconImage: () => null,
    });
    const win = trackWindow(manager.showSettingsWindow());
    await waitFor(() => win.webContents.executeJavaScript("typeof window.appSettings === 'object'", true));
    const snapshot = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript('window.appSettings.get()', true);
    assert.equal(snapshot.appLabel, `${appLabel} Fixture`);
    assert.equal(snapshot.values.theme, 'system');

    const invalid = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript("window.appSettings.validate({ theme: 'ultraviolet' })", true);
    assert.equal(invalid.valid, false);
    assert.ok(invalid.errors.theme);

    const saved = await win.webContents.executeJavaScript("window.appSettings.save({ theme: 'dark' })", true);
    assert.equal(saved.valid, true);
    assert.equal(saved.values.theme, 'dark');
    assert.equal(config.theme, 'dark');
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('closing a BrowserWindow aborts an active export and runs cleanup', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(null, `${fixture.origin}/export-close`);
    let cleanupRan = false;
    const job = new ExportJob({
      handlers: {
        locate: async currentJob => {
          currentJob.addCleanup(() => { cleanupRan = true; });
          await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            currentJob.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(currentJob.signal.reason), { once: true });
          });
        },
      },
    });
    const unbind = bindExportJobToWindow(job, win);
    const running = job.run();
    await delay(50);
    win.destroy();
    await assert.rejects(running, error => error instanceof ExportCancelledError && error.code === 'EXPORT_CANCELLED');
    unbind();
    assert.equal(cleanupRan, true);
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('capture cancellation crosses a real renderer RPC boundary', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(null, `${fixture.origin}/capture-cancel`);
    const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: `${appSlug}-electron-capture-cancel` });
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const onAbort = () => {
      if (!win.isDestroyed()) win.webContents.executeJavaScript('window.captureCancelled = true', true).catch(() => {});
    };
    controller.signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    try {
      const capture = captureConversation({
        store,
        signal: controller.signal,
        config: {
          exportCaptureSettleMs: 1,
          exportCaptureTerminalSamples: 2,
          exportCaptureBudgetMs: 5000,
          exportCaptureMaxSteps: 100,
        },
        callBatch: payload => win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`window.fixtureCaptureBatch(${JSON.stringify(payload)})`, true),
      });
      setTimeout(() => controller.abort('fixture cancellation'), 25);
      await assert.rejects(capture, error => error instanceof ExportCancelledError && error.code === 'EXPORT_CANCELLED');
    } finally {
      controller.signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
      await store.dispose();
    }
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('destroying a frame during an RPC returns a bounded structured failure', async () => {
    const win = await createFixtureWindow(null, `${fixture.origin}/with-frame?rev=rpc`);
    const frame = await waitFor(() => {
      const frames = win.webContents.mainFrame.framesInSubtree || [];
      return frames.find(candidate => candidate !== win.webContents.mainFrame && candidate.url.includes('/frame?rev=rpc'));
    });
    const rpc = callRendererMethodInFrame(
      win,
      frame.routingId,
      'slow',
      1000,
      { __rendererApiOptions: { rendererApiGlobal: '__rpcFixture' } }
    );
    await delay(50);
    await win.webContents.executeJavaScript("document.getElementById('same-origin-frame').remove()", true);
    const result = await Promise.race([
      rpc,
      new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Frame RPC did not settle after destruction.')), 3000)),
    ]);
    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.match(String(result.error || ''), /frame|context|destroy|disposed|available/i);
  }));

  cases.push(await runCase('file replacement preserves content and removes Windows backup artifacts', async () => {
    const directory = await fs.promises.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'atomic-replace-'));
    try {
      const destination = path.join(directory, 'export.txt');
      const temporary = path.join(directory, '.export.txt.pending.tmp');
      await fs.promises.writeFile(destination, 'old', 'utf8');
      await fs.promises.writeFile(temporary, 'new', 'utf8');
      const result = await replaceFileFromTemporary(temporary, destination, { platform: process.platform });
      assert.equal(await fs.promises.readFile(destination, 'utf8'), 'new');
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(temporary), false);
      assert.equal(result.cleanupError, null);
      const leftovers = (await fs.promises.readdir(directory)).filter(name => name.includes('replace-backup') || name.endsWith('.tmp'));
      assert.deepEqual(leftovers, []);
    } finally {
      await fs.promises.rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  }));

  await fixture.close();
  await destroyAllWindows();
  await fs.promises.rm(userData, { recursive: true, force: true });

  const failed = cases.filter(result => !result.ok);
  console.log(`1..${cases.length}`);
  console.log(`# ${cases.length - failed.length} passed; ${failed.length} failed`);
  app.exit(failed.length ? 1 : 0);
}

main().catch(async error => {
  console.error(error?.stack || error);
  await destroyAllWindows();
  app.exit(1);
});

===== END FILE: test/electron/integration.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/electron/packaged-smoke.js =====
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function parseReportPath(argv) {
  const prefix = '--smoke-report=';
  const arg = argv.find(value => String(value).startsWith(prefix));
  return arg ? path.resolve(String(arg).slice(prefix.length)) : path.join(process.cwd(), 'packaged-smoke-report.json');
}

function delay(ms) {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function waitFor(check, timeoutMs = 10000) {
  const started = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - started < timeoutMs) {
    const value = await check().catch(() => null);
    if (value) return value;
    await delay(25);
  }
  throw new Error(`Packaged smoke condition was not met within ${timeoutMs}ms.`);
}

async function runPackagedSmoke(deps) {
  const electron = require('electron');
  const app = deps.app || electron.app;
  const BrowserWindow = deps.BrowserWindow || electron.BrowserWindow;
  const ipcMain = deps.ipcMain || electron.ipcMain;
  const createExporters = deps.createExporters || require('../../lib/exporters').createExporters;
  const appConfig = deps.appConfig || require('../../app-specific/app.config');
  const chatDom = deps.chatDom || require('../../app-specific/chat-dom');
  const reportPath = parseReportPath(process.argv);
  const report = {
    platform: process.platform,
    packaged: app.isPackaged,
    startup: false,
    export: false,
    replacement: false,
  };
  let win = null;
  const rendererSettingsChannel = `${appConfig.appSlug}:renderer-settings`;
  const onRendererSettings = event => {
    event.returnValue = { directOpenBehavior: appConfig.defaultAppConfig.directOpenBehavior };
  };

  try {
    await app.whenReady();
    ipcMain.on(rendererSettingsChannel, onRendererSettings);
    win = new BrowserWindow({
      show: false,
      width: 800,
      height: 600,
      webPreferences: {
        nodeIntegration: false,
        contextIsolation: true,
        sandbox: false,
        preload: path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'app-specific', 'preload.js'),
      },
    });
    await win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, 'fixture', 'selection.html'));

    const globalName = String(appConfig.rendererApiGlobal || '__appRenderer');
    const version = Number(appConfig.rendererAgentVersion || 1);
    await waitFor(async () => win.webContents.executeJavaScript(
      `window[${JSON.stringify(globalName)}] && window[${JSON.stringify(globalName)}].__version === ${version}`,
      true
    ));
    report.startup = true;

    const exporters = createExporters({
      app,
      BrowserWindow,
      dialog: require('electron').dialog,
      safeShowError: (title, message) => { throw new Error(`${title}: ${message}`); },
      PRINT_BUBBLE_CSS: chatDom.PRINT_BUBBLE_CSS,
      CHAT_SCOPE_PSEUDO: chatDom.CHAT_SCOPE_PSEUDO,
      EXPORT_ROOT_CLASS: chatDom.EXPORT_ROOT_CLASS,
      EXPORT_ROOT_SELECTOR: chatDom.EXPORT_ROOT_SELECTOR,
      DOM_PRESERVE_CONTENT_SELECTORS: chatDom.DOM_PRESERVE_CONTENT_SELECTORS,
      getAppConfig: () => appConfig.defaultAppConfig,
      DEFAULT_APP_CONFIG: appConfig.defaultAppConfig,
      normalizeExportFormat: value => String(value || '').toLowerCase(),
      appLabel: appConfig.appLabel,
      appSlug: appConfig.appSlug,
      rendererApiGlobal: globalName,
    });

    const outputPath = path.join(app.getPath('temp'), `${appConfig.appSlug}-packaged-smoke-export.txt`);
    const selectFixture = text => win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`(() => {
      const node = document.getElementById('export-source');
      node.textContent = ${JSON.stringify(text)};
      const range = document.createRange();
      range.selectNodeContents(node);
      const selection = window.getSelection();
      selection.removeAllRanges();
      selection.addRange(range);
      return selection.toString();
    })()`, true);

    await selectFixture('Packaged export smoke one');
    await exporters.saveSelectionByProfile(win, 'plainText', outputPath);
    const first = await fs.promises.readFile(outputPath, 'utf8');
    if (first !== 'Packaged export smoke one') throw new Error('The first packaged export did not match the fixture selection.');
    report.export = true;

    await selectFixture('Packaged export smoke replacement');
    await exporters.saveSelectionByProfile(win, 'plainText', outputPath);
    const second = await fs.promises.readFile(outputPath, 'utf8');
    if (second !== 'Packaged export smoke replacement') throw new Error('The packaged export did not replace the existing file.');
    const leftovers = (await fs.promises.readdir(path.dirname(outputPath)))
      .filter(name => name.startsWith(`.${path.basename(outputPath)}.`) && (name.endsWith('.tmp') || name.includes('replace-backup')));
    if (leftovers.length) throw new Error(`Packaged export cleanup left artifacts: ${leftovers.join(', ')}`);
    report.replacement = true;
    report.outputBytes = Buffer.byteLength(second);
    await fs.promises.unlink(outputPath).catch(() => {});
  } catch (error) {
    report.error = String(error?.stack || error);
  } finally {
    try { if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.destroy(); } catch {}
    try { ipcMain.removeListener(rendererSettingsChannel, onRendererSettings); } catch {}
    await fs.promises.mkdir(path.dirname(reportPath), { recursive: true });
    await fs.promises.writeFile(reportPath, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2), 'utf8');
  }

  if (!report.startup || !report.export || !report.replacement) {
    console.error(report.error || 'Packaged smoke failed.');
    app.exit(1);
    return;
  }
  console.log(`PACKAGED_SMOKE_OK ${JSON.stringify(report)}`);
  app.exit(0);
}

module.exports = { runPackagedSmoke };

===== END FILE: test/electron/packaged-smoke.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/electron/run-packaged-smoke.js =====
'use strict';

const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');

function executablePath(root) {
  const packageJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
  const productName = String(packageJson.build?.productName || packageJson.name);
  const executableName = String(packageJson.build?.linux?.executableName || packageJson.name);
  if (process.platform === 'win32') {
    return path.join(root, 'dist', 'win-unpacked', `${productName}.exe`);
  }
  return path.join(root, 'dist', 'linux-unpacked', executableName);
}

async function main() {
  const root = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
  const executable = executablePath(root);
  assert.equal(fs.existsSync(executable), true, `Packaged executable not found: ${executable}`);

  const tempDirectory = await fs.promises.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'copilot-packaged-smoke-'));
  const reportPath = path.join(tempDirectory, 'report.json');
  const args = ['--packaged-smoke', `--smoke-report=${reportPath}`];
  if (process.platform === 'linux' && typeof process.getuid === 'function' && process.getuid() === 0) {
    args.unshift('--no-sandbox');
  }

  const exitCode = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(executable, args, {
      cwd: root,
      stdio: 'inherit',
      env: {
        ...process.env,
        ELECTRON_DISABLE_SECURITY_WARNINGS: 'true',
      },
    });
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      child.kill('SIGKILL');
      reject(new Error('Packaged smoke process timed out.'));
    }, 30000);
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('exit', code => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      resolve(code);
    });
  });

  assert.equal(exitCode, 0, 'Packaged application exited with a failure code.');
  const report = JSON.parse(await fs.promises.readFile(reportPath, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(report.packaged, true);
  assert.equal(report.startup, true);
  assert.equal(report.export, true);
  assert.equal(report.replacement, true);
  console.log(`Packaged ${process.platform} startup/export smoke passed.`);
  await fs.promises.rm(tempDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

main().catch(error => {
  console.error(error?.stack || error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

===== END FILE: test/electron/run-packaged-smoke.js =====

'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const {
  evaluateSnapshotCompleteness,
  resolveExportPaperPalette,
} = require('../lib/export-validation');

test('completeness gate requires every known live-turn fingerprint', () => {
  const result = evaluateSnapshotCompleteness(
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'b',
      messageFingerprints: ['a', 'b'],
    },
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 1,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'a',
      messageFingerprints: ['a'],
      monotonicRowOrdering: true,
    },
    {},
    { bottomCoverageGapPx: 1200 }
  );

  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, true);
  assert.equal(result.logicalTurnsBefore, 2);
  assert.equal(result.logicalTurnsAfter, 1);
  assert.equal(result.missingFingerprintCount, 1);
  assert.equal(result.fingerprintCoveragePct, 50);
  assert.match(result.warnings.join('\n'), /Captured 1 of 2 logical turns/);
  assert.match(result.warnings.join('\n'), /fingerprint/);
});

test('completeness gate accepts an ordered exact static capture', () => {
  const result = evaluateSnapshotCompleteness(
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'b',
      messageFingerprints: ['a', 'b'],
      bottomCoverageGap: 0,
    },
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'b',
      messageFingerprints: ['a', 'b'],
      monotonicRowOrdering: true,
      bottomCoverageGap: 0,
      missingImageCount: 0,
    },
    {},
    { bottomCoverageGapPx: 1200 }
  );

  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, false);
  assert.equal(result.missingFingerprintCount, 0);
  assert.equal(result.fingerprintCoveragePct, 100);
  assert.deepEqual(result.warnings, []);
});

test('completeness gate uses stable logical anchors when expanded content changes fingerprints', () => {
  const result = evaluateSnapshotCompleteness(
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'before-a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'before-b',
      messageFingerprints: ['before-a', 'before-b'],
      firstRowLogicalAnchor: 'source:message:a',
      lastRowLogicalAnchor: 'source:message:b',
      logicalAnchors: ['source:message:a', 'source:message:b'],
    },
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      firstRowFingerprint: 'expanded-a',
      lastRowFingerprint: 'expanded-b',
      messageFingerprints: ['expanded-a', 'expanded-b'],
      firstRowLogicalAnchor: 'source:message:a',
      lastRowLogicalAnchor: 'source:message:b',
      logicalAnchors: ['source:message:a', 'source:message:b'],
      monotonicRowOrdering: true,
    }
  );

  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, false);
  assert.equal(result.missingFingerprintCount, 0);
  assert.equal(result.missingLogicalAnchorCount, 0);
  assert.equal(result.logicalAnchorCoveragePct, 100);
  assert.deepEqual(result.warnings, []);
});

test('completeness gate rejects a missing logical anchor even when content fingerprints match', () => {
  const result = evaluateSnapshotCompleteness(
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      messageFingerprints: ['same', 'same'],
      logicalAnchors: ['source:message:a', 'source:message:b'],
    },
    {
      logicalTurnCount: 2,
      messageFingerprints: ['same', 'same'],
      logicalAnchors: ['source:message:a', 'source:message:c'],
      monotonicRowOrdering: true,
    }
  );

  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, true);
  assert.equal(result.missingLogicalAnchorCount, 1);
  assert.equal(result.logicalAnchorCoveragePct, 50);
  assert.match(result.warnings.join('\n'), /logical turn anchor/);
});

test('completeness gate rejects an empty capture even without a live baseline', () => {
  const result = evaluateSnapshotCompleteness({}, {}, {}, { bottomCoverageGapPx: 1200 });
  assert.equal(result.lowConfidence, true);
  assert.match(result.warnings.join('\n'), /No logical conversation turns/);
});

test('match-paper palette repairs light-on-light transparent-root fallback', () => {
  const palette = resolveExportPaperPalette({
    foreground: 'rgb(243, 244, 246)',
    background: '#ffffff',
    prefersDark: true,
    colorScheme: 'dark',
  }, 'match');

  assert.equal(palette.foreground, '#f3f4f6');
  assert.equal(palette.background, '#1f1f1f');
  assert.equal(palette.colorScheme, 'dark');
});

'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const {
  EXPORT_JOB_PHASES,
  ExportJob,
  ExportCancelledError,
  ConversationSnapshot,
  ConversationSnapshotCache,
} = require('../lib/export-job');

test('ExportJob executes the required phases in order and always cleans up', async () => {
  const phases = [];
  const cleanup = [];
  const handlers = Object.fromEntries(EXPORT_JOB_PHASES.map(phase => [phase, async job => {
    phases.push(phase);
    if (phase === 'locate') job.addCleanup(async () => cleanup.push('restored'));
    return { phase };
  }]));
  const job = new ExportJob({ handlers });
  const result = await job.run();
  assert.equal(result.ok, true);
  assert.deepEqual(phases, EXPORT_JOB_PHASES);
  assert.deepEqual(cleanup, ['restored']);
  assert.equal(result.context instanceof Object, true);
});

test('ExportJob cancellation rejects and still runs cleanup', async () => {
  const cleanup = [];
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const job = new ExportJob({
    controller,
    handlers: {
      locate: async current => {
        current.addCleanup(() => cleanup.push('clean'));
        controller.abort('stop');
      },
    },
  });
  await assert.rejects(job.run(), error => error instanceof ExportCancelledError);
  assert.deepEqual(cleanup, ['clean']);
});

test('ConversationSnapshotCache keys entries by owner, revision, identity and expansion options', () => {
  const cache = new ConversationSnapshotCache();
  const owner = {};
  const identity = {
    conversationIdentity: 'conversation-1',
    url: 'https://example.test/chat/1',
    mutationRevision: 7,
    expansionOptions: { expandReasoning: true },
    includeRaw: false,
  };
  const snapshot = new ConversationSnapshot({
    cleanSemanticHtml: '<article>ok</article>',
    plainText: 'ok',
    title: 'Conversation',
  });
  cache.set(owner, identity, snapshot);
  assert.equal(cache.get(owner, identity), snapshot);
  assert.equal(cache.get(owner, { ...identity, mutationRevision: 8 }), null);
  assert.equal(cache.get(owner, { ...identity, expansionOptions: { expandReasoning: false } }), null);
  cache.invalidate(owner);
  assert.equal(cache.get(owner, identity), null);
});

test('ConversationSnapshot preserves the reusable capture fields', () => {
  const snapshot = new ConversationSnapshot({
    cleanSemanticHtml: '<main>clean</main>',
    rawHtml: '<main class="raw">clean</main>',
    plainText: 'clean',
    messageFingerprints: ['1:5'],
    orderFingerprints: ['1:5'],
    title: 'Title',
    url: 'https://example.test/chat',
    capturedAt: '2026-10-08T16:00:00.000Z',
    theme: { foreground: '#111', background: '#fff' },
    sourceMetadata: { rendererAgentVersion: 3 },
    materializedImageMap: { 'https://example.test/a.png': 'data:image/png;base64,AA==' },
    completeness: { logicalTurnsAfter: 1, lowConfidence: false },
  });
  assert.equal(snapshot.cleanSemanticHtml, '<main>clean</main>');
  assert.equal(snapshot.rawHtml.includes('raw'), true);
  assert.equal(snapshot.plainText, 'clean');
  assert.deepEqual(snapshot.messageFingerprints, ['1:5']);
  assert.equal(snapshot.completeness.lowConfidence, false);
  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(snapshot), true);
});

===== BEGIN FILE: test/export-progress.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const os = require('os');

const {
  CANCEL_HEADLINE,
  CANCEL_FOOTER,
  ACTIVE_FOOTER,
  formatDuration,
  formatBar,
  buildExportProgressView,
} = require('../lib/export-progress');
const { ExportJob, ExportCancelledError } = require('../lib/export-job');
const { createCanonicalConversationStore } = require('../lib/canonical-conversation');
const { captureConversation } = require('../lib/conversation-capture');
const { createHarness } = require('../test-support/dom-agent-harness');

test('formatDuration and formatBar produce stable text', () => {
  assert.equal(formatDuration(0), '0:00');
  assert.equal(formatDuration(65_400), '1:05');
  assert.equal(formatDuration(3_723_000), '1:02:03');
  assert.equal(formatBar(0.5), `${'\u2588'.repeat(10)}${'\u2591'.repeat(10)} 50%`);
  assert.equal(formatBar(2), `${'\u2588'.repeat(20)} 100%`);
  assert.equal(formatBar(NaN), '');
});

test('capture view shows captured/expected turns, pass, backtracks and elapsed time', () => {
  const view = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'capture',
    phaseIndex: 3,
    phaseCount: 8,
    message: 'Capturing conversation',
    elapsedMs: 83_000,
    metrics: { pass: 'capture', capturedTurns: 120, expectedTurns: 300, backtracks: 4, scrollPercent: 38 },
  });
  assert.deepEqual(view.lines, [
    'Capturing conversation',
    'Step 4 of 8: Capturing conversation \u00b7 1:23 elapsed',
    'Turns: 120 captured of 300 expected',
    'Pass: initial capture',
    'Position 38% \u00b7 4 backtracks',
    formatBar(0.4),
    ACTIVE_FOOTER,
  ]);
});

test('verification pass and unknown totals are labelled explicitly', () => {
  const view = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'capture',
    message: 'Verifying no turns were missed',
    metrics: {
      pass: 'verify', verificationPass: 1, maxVerificationPasses: 2,
      capturedTurns: 57, backtracks: 1, continuityBreaks: 0, scrollPercent: 90,
    },
  });
  assert.ok(view.lines.includes('Turns: 57 captured (total not reported by page)'));
  assert.ok(view.lines.includes('Pass: verification 1 of up to 2'));
  assert.ok(view.lines.includes('Position 90% \u00b7 1 backtrack'));
  assert.equal(view.fraction, 0.9);
});

test('transform and render views show assets and PDF shard numbers', () => {
  const transform = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'transform',
    metrics: {
      serializedTurns: 40, totalTurns: 160,
      assetsProcessed: 12, assetsEmbedded: 10, assetsFailed: 2, pdfShard: 2,
    },
  });
  assert.ok(transform.lines.includes('Converted: 40 of 160 turns'));
  assert.ok(transform.lines.includes('Images: 12 processed (10 saved, 2 failed)'));
  assert.ok(transform.lines.includes('PDF section: 2 being assembled'));
  assert.equal(transform.fraction, 0.25);

  const render = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'render',
    metrics: { pdfShard: 3, pdfShards: 7, renderedPages: 41, assetsProcessed: 12, assetsEmbedded: 12 },
  });
  assert.ok(render.lines.includes('PDF section: 3 of 7 \u00b7 41 pages'));
  assert.ok(render.lines.includes('Images: 12 processed (12 saved)'));
});

test('cancelling view replaces the Esc hint with a finishing-current-turn state', () => {
  const view = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'transform',
    message: 'Converting turns',
    cancelling: true,
    metrics: { serializedTurns: 5, totalTurns: 10 },
  });
  assert.equal(view.lines[0], CANCEL_HEADLINE);
  assert.equal(view.lines[1], 'Converting turns');
  assert.equal(view.lines[view.lines.length - 1], CANCEL_FOOTER);
  assert.ok(!view.lines.includes(ACTIVE_FOOTER));
  assert.ok(!view.lines.some(line => line.includes('%')), 'no progress bar while cancelling');
});

test('ExportJob keeps metrics sticky, throttles reports and reuses the last message', async () => {
  const reports = [];
  const job = new ExportJob({
    progressIntervalMs: 10_000,
    reportProgress: async payload => { reports.push(payload); },
    handlers: {
      capture: async current => {
        await current.reportMetrics('Capturing conversation', { capturedTurns: 1 }, { force: true });
        await current.reportMetrics('Capturing conversation', { capturedTurns: 2, backtracks: 1 });
        assert.equal(current.metrics.capturedTurns, 2, 'metrics merge even when the report is throttled');
        await current.updateProgress();
      },
    },
  });
  await job.run();
  const capture = reports.filter(report => report.phase === 'capture');
  const forced = capture.find(report => report.metrics.capturedTurns === 1);
  assert.ok(forced, 'forced report was sent');
  const heartbeatLike = capture.find(report => report.metrics.capturedTurns === 2 && report.metrics.backtracks === 1);
  assert.ok(heartbeatLike, 'later report carries throttled metrics');
  assert.equal(heartbeatLike.message, 'Capturing conversation');
  assert.equal(heartbeatLike.phaseIndex, 3);
  assert.equal(heartbeatLike.phaseCount, 8);
  assert.ok(Array.isArray(heartbeatLike.lines) && heartbeatLike.lines.includes('Turns: 2 captured (total not reported by page)'));
});

test('ExportJob publishes the cancelling state immediately on abort', async () => {
  const reports = [];
  let release;
  const job = new ExportJob({
    reportProgress: async payload => { reports.push(payload); },
    handlers: {
      transform: async current => {
        await current.reportMetrics('Converting turns', { serializedTurns: 3, totalTurns: 9 }, { force: true });
        current.abort('user');
        // Simulate the in-flight turn finishing after the abort was requested.
        await new Promise(resolve => { release = resolve; setTimeout(resolve, 5); });
        current.throwIfCancelled();
      },
    },
  });
  await assert.rejects(job.run(), error => error instanceof ExportCancelledError);
  const cancelling = reports.find(report => report.cancelling === true);
  assert.ok(cancelling, 'a cancelling report was emitted');
  assert.equal(cancelling.phase, 'transform');
  assert.equal(cancelling.cancellable, false);
  assert.equal(cancelling.lines[0], CANCEL_HEADLINE);
  assert.ok(cancelling.lines.includes('Converted: 3 of 9 turns'));
  assert.ok(typeof release === 'function');
});

// Minimal virtualizer: fixed heights, optional ordinals, and an optional
// forced continuity break that produces one backtrack per pass.
function makePage({ count, height = 300, viewport = 800, ordinals = false, breakOnce = false }) {
  let scrollTop = 0;
  let broke = false;
  const sent = new Set();
  const total = count * height;
  const range = () => Math.max(0, total - viewport);
  return {
    async callBatch(opts) {
      for (const key of opts.resend || []) sent.delete(key);
      scrollTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(range(), Number(opts.target) || 0));
      let view = scrollTop;
      if (breakOnce && !broke && !opts.observeOnly && scrollTop > 0) {
        broke = true;
        view = Math.min(range(), scrollTop + viewport * 3);
      }
      const refs = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
        const y = i * height;
        if (y + height > view && y < view + viewport) {
          refs.push({
            key: `t${i + 1}`, sourceKey: `message:t${i + 1}`, role: 'unknown', fingerprint: `t${i + 1}`,
            textLength: 10, ...(ordinals ? { ordinal: i + 1, setSize: count } : {}),
          });
        }
      }
      const records = opts.observeOnly ? [] : refs
        .filter(ref => !sent.has(ref.key))
        .map(ref => (sent.add(ref.key), { ...ref, html: `<p>${ref.key}</p>` }));
      return {
        ok: true, refs, records, scrollTop, clientHeight: viewport, range: range(),
        atTop: scrollTop <= 2, atBottom: scrollTop >= range() - 2,
      };
    },
  };
}

async function captureWithProgress(page, config = {}) {
  const store = await createCanonicalConversationStore({ tempRoot: os.tmpdir(), prefix: 'progress-test' });
  const events = [];
  try {
    await captureConversation({
      store,
      callBatch: page.callBatch,
      progressIntervalMs: 0,
      onProgress: info => { events.push(info); },
      config: { exportCaptureSettleMs: 1, ...config },
    });
    return { events, manifest: store.buildManifest() };
  } finally {
    await store.dispose();
  }
}

test('capture progress reports canonical-store turns and the page-reported total', async () => {
  const { events, manifest } = await captureWithProgress(makePage({ count: 30, ordinals: true }));
  assert.equal(manifest.turnCount, 30);
  const last = events[events.length - 1];
  assert.equal(last.passComplete, true);
  assert.equal(last.capturedTurns, 30);
  assert.equal(last.expectedTurns, 30);
  assert.equal(last.pass, 'capture');
  for (let i = 1; i < events.length; i += 1) {
    assert.ok(events[i].capturedTurns >= events[i - 1].capturedTurns, 'captured turns never decrease');
  }
});

test('capture progress numbers verification passes and accumulates backtracks', async () => {
  const { events } = await captureWithProgress(makePage({ count: 30, breakOnce: true }), {
    exportCaptureVerificationPasses: 2,
  });
  const verify = events.filter(event => event.pass === 'verify');
  assert.ok(verify.length > 0, 'verification pass reported');
  assert.equal(verify[0].verificationPass, 1);
  assert.equal(verify[0].maxVerificationPasses, 2);
  const captureBacktracks = Math.max(...events.filter(e => e.pass === 'capture').map(e => e.backtracks));
  assert.ok(captureBacktracks >= 1, 'backtrack counted during capture');
  assert.ok(verify.every(event => event.backtracks >= captureBacktracks), 'backtracks are cumulative across passes');
});

test('renderer overlay paints lines and keeps the cancelling state sticky', t => {
  const harness = createHarness('empty-shell.json', {});
  t.after(() => harness.dispose());
  const { api, document } = harness;
  const overlay = () => document.querySelector('[data-app-expand-overlay]');
  const active = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'capture', message: 'Capturing conversation', metrics: { capturedTurns: 3 },
  });

  assert.equal(api.beginExportJob({ jobId: 'job-1' }).ok, true);
  api.updateExportJobProgress({ jobId: 'job-1', message: active.message, lines: active.lines });
  assert.equal(overlay().textContent, active.lines.join('\n'));

  api.cancelExportJob({ jobId: 'job-1' });
  const afterCancel = overlay().textContent.split('\n');
  assert.equal(afterCancel[0], CANCEL_HEADLINE);
  assert.equal(afterCancel[afterCancel.length - 1], CANCEL_FOOTER);
  assert.ok(afterCancel.includes('Turns: 3 captured (total not reported by page)'));

  // A progress message that was already in flight (not yet cancelling) must
  // not restore the "Press Esc to cancel" presentation.
  const stale = buildExportProgressView({
    phase: 'capture', message: 'Capturing conversation', metrics: { capturedTurns: 4 },
  });
  api.updateExportJobProgress({ jobId: 'job-1', message: stale.message, lines: stale.lines });
  const painted = overlay().textContent.split('\n');
  assert.equal(painted[0], CANCEL_HEADLINE);
  assert.ok(!painted.includes(ACTIVE_FOOTER));
  assert.ok(painted.includes('Turns: 4 captured (total not reported by page)'));

  api.endExportJob({ jobId: 'job-1' });
  assert.equal(overlay(), null, 'overlay removed when the job ends');
});

===== END FILE: test/export-progress.test.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/renderer-agent-installer.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { EventEmitter } = require('events');
const { createRendererAgentInstaller } = require('../lib/renderer-agent-installer');

class FakeExecutor extends EventEmitter {
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.sources = [];
    this.destroyed = false;
  }
  isDestroyed() { return this.destroyed; }
  async executeJavaScript(source) {
    this.sources.push(source);
    return null;
  }
}

test('boot source puts configured global and version before the agent source', () => {
  const installer = createRendererAgentInstaller({
    fs: { readFileSync: () => '(function(){ window.agentSaw = window.__APP_RENDERER_AGENT_CONFIG__; })();' },
    agentPath: '/fixture/agent.js',
    rendererApiGlobal: '__configuredRenderer',
    rendererAgentVersion: 17,
    agentConfig: { chatRootSelectors: ['#chat'] },
  });
  const source = installer.getBootSource();
  assert.ok(source.indexOf('__APP_RENDERER_AGENT_CONFIG__') < source.indexOf('window.agentSaw'));
  assert.match(source, /__configuredRenderer/);
  assert.match(source, /rendererAgentVersion[^\n]*17/);
  assert.match(source, /chatRootSelectors/);
});

test('installer executes for top-level and frame dom-ready events and detaches on destruction', async () => {
  const webContents = new FakeExecutor();
  webContents.mainFrame = {};
  const frame = new FakeExecutor();
  const installer = createRendererAgentInstaller({
    fs: { readFileSync: () => 'window.fixtureAgent = true;' },
    agentPath: '/fixture/agent.js',
  });
  installer.install(webContents);

  webContents.emit('dom-ready');
  webContents.emit('frame-created', {}, { frame });
  frame.emit('dom-ready');
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  assert.equal(webContents.sources.length, 1);
  assert.equal(frame.sources.length, 1);

  webContents.destroyed = true;
  webContents.emit('destroyed');
  webContents.emit('dom-ready');
  frame.emit('dom-ready');
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  assert.equal(webContents.sources.length, 1);
  assert.equal(frame.sources.length, 1);
});

===== END FILE: test/renderer-agent-installer.test.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/run-unit-tests.js =====
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');

const testDirectory = __dirname;
const files = fs.readdirSync(testDirectory)
  .filter(name => name.endsWith('.test.js'))
  .sort()
  .map(name => path.join(testDirectory, name));

const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--test', ...files], {
  cwd: path.join(__dirname, '..'),
  stdio: 'inherit',
  env: process.env,
});

if (result.error) throw result.error;
process.exitCode = result.status === null ? 1 : result.status;

===== END FILE: test/run-unit-tests.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/scroller-discovery.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const {
  createHarness,
  deepClone,
} = require('../test-support/dom-agent-harness');

const SCROLLER_CONTRACT = {
  version: 1,
  thresholds: { read: 55, interact: 70, destructive: 85 },
  negativeRegionSelectors: [],
  capabilities: {
    chatRoot: {
      selectors: ['[data-chat-root]'],
      semanticSelectors: ['[role="feed"]'],
      repeatedDescendants: ['article'],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
    messageRow: {
      selectors: ['article'],
      semanticSelectors: ['article'],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
    composer: {
      selectors: [],
      semanticSelectors: [],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
    disclosureControl: {
      selectors: [],
      semanticSelectors: [],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
    virtualizer: {
      selectors: ['[data-virtualizer]'],
      stableAttributeHints: [],
    },
  },
};

function scrollerAgentConfig({ virtualizerSelectors = ['[data-virtualizer]'] } = {}) {
  const contract = deepClone(SCROLLER_CONTRACT);
  contract.capabilities.virtualizer.selectors = virtualizerSelectors.slice();
  return {
    chatRootSelectors: contract.capabilities.chatRoot.selectors.slice(),
    virtualizerSelectors: virtualizerSelectors.slice(),
    chatInputSelectors: [],
    scoreRules: [],
    collectionSelectors: ['article'],
    collectionKeyAttributes: ['id'],
    collectionRowSelectors: ['article'],
    collectionExcludeSelectors: [],
    domAdapterContract: contract,
  };
}

function useHarness(t, fixture, options = {}) {
  const harness = createHarness(fixture, options);
  t.after(() => harness.dispose());
  return harness;
}

function trackDocumentSelectors(document) {
  const originalQuerySelectorAll = document.querySelectorAll.bind(document);
  const selectors = [];
  document.querySelectorAll = selector => {
    selectors.push(String(selector));
    return originalQuerySelectorAll(selector);
  };
  return selectors;
}

function qualifiedScrollerFixture(rootId = 'conversation-a', scrollerId = 'virtualizer-a') {
  return {
    body: {
      children: [{
        tag: 'div',
        attrs: {
          id: scrollerId,
          'data-virtualizer': '1',
          'data-overflow-y': 'auto',
        },
        scroll: { scrollHeight: 2200, clientHeight: 500 },
        children: [{
          tag: 'div',
          attrs: { id: rootId, role: 'feed', 'data-chat-root': '1' },
          children: [
            { tag: 'article', attrs: { id: `${rootId}-m1` }, text: 'One' },
            { tag: 'article', attrs: { id: `${rootId}-m2` }, text: 'Two' },
          ],
        }],
      }],
    },
  };
}

test('qualified scroller candidates bypass the generic document scan and remain cached across mutations', async t => {
  const harness = useHarness(t, qualifiedScrollerFixture(), { config: scrollerAgentConfig() });
  const { api, document } = harness;
  const selectors = trackDocumentSelectors(document);

  const first = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.equal(first.ok, true);
  assert.equal(first.scrollerFound, true);
  assert.match(first.scrollerLabel, /#virtualizer-a/);
  assert.equal(selectors.includes('body *'), false);

  let health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.genericScanCount, 0);
  assert.ok(health.scrollerDiscovery.qualifiedCandidateHits >= 1);
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.fullDocumentFallbackNeeded, false);
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.selectionSource, 'qualified-rescan');
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.cacheState, 'cached');

  document.body.appendChild(harness.createElement({
    tag: 'span',
    attrs: { id: 'unrelated-mutation' },
    text: 'Unrelated mutation',
  }));
  await harness.settle();
  selectors.length = 0;

  const second = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.match(second.scrollerLabel, /#virtualizer-a/);
  assert.equal(selectors.includes('body *'), false);
  health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  assert.ok(health.scrollerDiscovery.cacheHits >= 1);
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.genericScanCount, 0);
});

test('cached scroller invalidates when the selected chat root changes', async t => {
  const harness = useHarness(t, qualifiedScrollerFixture(), { config: scrollerAgentConfig() });
  const { api, document } = harness;

  const first = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.match(first.scrollerLabel, /#virtualizer-a/);

  document.getElementById('virtualizer-a').remove();
  document.body.appendChild(harness.createElement(
    qualifiedScrollerFixture('conversation-b', 'virtualizer-b').body.children[0]
  ));
  await harness.settle();

  const second = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.equal(second.ok, true);
  assert.match(second.scrollerLabel, /#virtualizer-b/);
});

test('cached scroller invalidates when it disconnects while the chat root remains', async t => {
  const fixture = {
    body: {
      children: [{
        tag: 'div',
        attrs: { id: 'stable-root', role: 'feed', 'data-chat-root': '1' },
        children: [{
          tag: 'div',
          attrs: {
            id: 'inner-virtualizer-a',
            'data-virtualizer': '1',
            'data-overflow-y': 'auto',
          },
          scroll: { scrollHeight: 1800, clientHeight: 400 },
          children: [{ tag: 'article', attrs: { id: 'message-a' }, text: 'One' }],
        }],
      }],
    },
  };
  const harness = useHarness(t, fixture, { config: scrollerAgentConfig() });
  const { api, document } = harness;

  const first = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.match(first.scrollerLabel, /#inner-virtualizer-a/);

  document.getElementById('inner-virtualizer-a').remove();
  document.getElementById('stable-root').appendChild(harness.createElement({
    tag: 'div',
    attrs: {
      id: 'inner-virtualizer-b',
      'data-virtualizer': '1',
      'data-overflow-y': 'auto',
    },
    scroll: { scrollHeight: 1900, clientHeight: 400 },
    children: [{ tag: 'article', attrs: { id: 'message-b' }, text: 'Two' }],
  }));
  await harness.settle();

  const second = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.equal(second.ok, true);
  assert.match(second.scrollerLabel, /#inner-virtualizer-b/);
});

test('generic document scan remains an observable final fallback', t => {
  const fixture = {
    body: {
      children: [
        {
          tag: 'div',
          attrs: { id: 'fallback-root', role: 'feed', 'data-chat-root': '1' },
          children: [{ tag: 'article', attrs: { id: 'fallback-message' }, text: 'One' }],
        },
        {
          tag: 'div',
          attrs: { id: 'generic-only-scroller', 'data-overflow-y': 'auto' },
          scroll: { scrollHeight: 1600, clientHeight: 300 },
        },
      ],
    },
  };
  const harness = useHarness(t, fixture, {
    config: scrollerAgentConfig({ virtualizerSelectors: [] }),
  });
  const { api, document } = harness;
  const selectors = trackDocumentSelectors(document);

  const measurement = api.measureChatScroller({ fallbackSelector: '[data-chat-root]' });
  assert.equal(measurement.ok, true);
  assert.equal(measurement.scrollerFound, true);
  assert.match(measurement.scrollerLabel, /#generic-only-scroller/);
  assert.equal(selectors.includes('body *'), true);

  const health = api.getDomAdapterHealth();
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.genericScanCount, 1);
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.fullDocumentFallbackNeeded, true);
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.selectionSource, 'full-document-rescan');
  assert.equal(health.scrollerDiscovery.cacheState, 'cached');
});

===== END FILE: test/scroller-discovery.test.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: test/selector-health.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const {
  buildSelectorHealthReport,
  formatCapabilityLine,
  formatScrollerDiscoveryLines,
  statusForCapability,
} = require('../lib/selector-health');

test('Selector Health reports when the generic document scan was unnecessary', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    formatScrollerDiscoveryLines({
      cacheHits: 7,
      cacheMisses: 1,
      qualifiedCandidateHits: 1,
      genericScanCount: 0,
      cachedMutationGeneration: 12,
    }),
    [
      'Scroller cache: 7 hits, 1 miss',
      'Qualified scroller candidates: 1',
      'Generic document scan fallback: not required',
      'Cached mutation generation: 12',
    ]
  );
});

test('Selector Health reports each required generic document scan', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    formatScrollerDiscoveryLines({ genericScanCount: 3 }),
    [
      'Scroller cache: 0 hits, 0 misses',
      'Qualified scroller candidates: 0',
      'Generic document scan fallback: required 3 times',
      'Cached mutation generation: 0',
    ]
  );
});

test('Selector Health formats the four actionable capability states', () => {
  assert.equal(statusForCapability({ status: 'Healthy' }), 'Healthy');
  assert.equal(statusForCapability({ status: 'Fallback' }), 'Fallback');
  assert.equal(statusForCapability({ found: true, confidence: 40, confidenceThreshold: 55 }), 'Low confidence');
  assert.equal(statusForCapability({ found: false, confidence: 0, confidenceThreshold: 55 }), 'Missing');
  assert.equal(
    formatCapabilityLine('composer', {
      status: 'Fallback',
      confidence: 78,
      confidenceThreshold: 70,
      requiredLevel: 'interact',
      selectedTier: 'semantic-structural',
    }),
    'Composer: Fallback | tier semantic-structural | confidence 78/70 (interact)'
  );
});

test('diagnostic report is actionable and excludes conversation text', () => {
  const report = buildSelectorHealthReport({
    appLabel: 'Fixture App',
    appVersion: '1.2.3',
    rendererAgentVersion: 6,
    urlOrigin: 'https://example.test',
    frameCount: 3,
    respondingFrameCount: 2,
    selectedFrame: 'frame:42',
    diagnosticSession: { active: false },
    health: {
      selectedRoot: 'main#conversation',
      activeFallbackTier: 'semantic-structural',
      messageRowCount: 2,
      contractVersion: 1,
      capabilities: {
        chatRoot: { status: 'Healthy', confidence: 90, confidenceThreshold: 55, requiredLevel: 'read', selectedTier: 'structural-stable' },
        messageRow: { status: 'Healthy', confidence: 80, confidenceThreshold: 55, requiredLevel: 'read', selectedTier: 'structural-stable' },
        composer: { status: 'Fallback', confidence: 75, confidenceThreshold: 70, requiredLevel: 'interact', selectedTier: 'semantic-structural' },
        disclosureControl: { status: 'Low confidence', confidence: 60, confidenceThreshold: 70, requiredLevel: 'interact', selectedTier: 'structural' },
        virtualizer: { status: 'Missing', confidence: 0, confidenceThreshold: 70, requiredLevel: 'interact', selectedTier: 'none' },
      },

  });

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');

function fixture(t, injected = fs) {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'configuration-recovery-'));
  const runtime = createRuntimeConfig({ app: { getPath: () => dir }, fs: injected, path,
    defaultAppConfig: { ...defaults, enableConsoleLogging: true } });
  t.after(async () => { await runtime.shutdownLogging(); fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); });
  runtime.loadAppConfig();
  return { runtime, dir, file: runtime.getConfigFilePath() };

test('previous valid config survives no-op save and startup', t => {

});

  assert.throws(() => f.runtime.updateAppConfig({ theme: 'dark' }), /write failure/);

  const f = fixture(t, injected); const before = fs.readFileSync(f.file, 'utf8'); fail = true;

});

===== BEGIN FILE: test/settings-validation-concurrency.test.js =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const vm = require('node:vm');

function deferred() {
  let resolve, reject;
  const promise = new Promise((yes, no) => { resolve = yes; reject = no; });
  return { promise, resolve, reject };
}

async function fixture() {
  const nodes = new Map();
  function node(id) {
    if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, {
      textContent: '', className: '', disabled: false, hidden: false,
      listeners: {},
      attributes: {},
      setAttribute(name, value) { this.attributes[name] = value; },
      getAttribute(name) { return this.attributes[name]; },
      querySelector() { return null; },
      focus() { this.focused = true; },
      addEventListener(type, handler) { this.listeners[type] = handler; },
      querySelectorAll() { return []; },
      replaceChildren() {},
    });
    return nodes.get(id);
  }
  const requests = [], saves = [], timers = new Map();
  let timerId = 0;
  const context = vm.createContext({
    document: { getElementById: node, querySelectorAll: () => [] },
    window: {
      addEventListener() {},
      appSettings: {
        get: async () => ({ values: { theme: 'system' }, schema: [] }),
        validate(patch) {
          const request = { ...deferred(), patch };
          requests.push(request);
          return request.promise;
        },
        save(patch) {
          const request = { ...deferred(), patch };
          saves.push(request);
          return request.promise;
        },
      },
    },
    setTimeout(callback, delay) {
      const id = ++timerId;
      timers.set(id, { callback, delay });
      return id;
    },
    clearTimeout(id) { timers.delete(id); },
  });
  const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '../renderer/settings.js'), 'utf8');
  vm.runInContext(source + '\nglobalThis.harness = { state, validate, scheduleValidation, restoreBaseline };', context);
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  requests[0].resolve({ errors: {}, effects: [] });
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  requests.length = 0;
  return {
    context,
    ...context.harness, requests, saves, timers, node,
    submit: () => node('settings-form').listeners.submit({ preventDefault() {} }),
    fireTimer() {
      const [id, timer] = timers.entries().next().value;
      timers.delete(id);
      timer.callback();
    },
  };
}

const current = { errors: { theme: 'Current error' }, effects: [{ label: 'Theme', effect: 'reload' }] };
const stale = { errors: { _form: 'Stale error' }, effects: [{ label: 'Old', effect: 'next-launch' }] };
function snapshot(f) {
  return JSON.stringify({ errors: f.state.errors, effects: f.state.effects,
    message: f.node('form-message').textContent, saveDisabled: f.node('save').disabled,
    summary: f.node('effect-summary').textContent });
}

test('older successful validation cannot overwrite a newer result', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const older = f.validate(), newer = f.validate();
  f.requests[1].resolve(current); await newer;
  const expected = snapshot(f);
  f.requests[0].resolve(stale); await older;
  assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
});

test('older rejected validation cannot overwrite a newer result', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const older = f.validate(), newer = f.validate();
  f.requests[1].resolve(current); await newer;
  const expected = snapshot(f);
  f.requests[0].reject(new Error('Stale rejection')); await older;
  assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
});

test('editing invalidates a response before the next debounce fires', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const pending = f.validate();
  f.state.values.theme = 'dark'; f.scheduleValidation();
  const expected = snapshot(f);
  f.requests[0].resolve(stale); await pending;
  assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
  assert.equal(f.timers.size, 1);
});

test('120 ms debounce coalesces edits and clears its timer reference', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  f.scheduleValidation(); f.scheduleValidation();
  assert.equal(f.timers.size, 1);
  assert.equal([...f.timers.values()][0].delay, 120);
  f.fireTimer();
  assert.equal(f.state.validationTimer, null);
  assert.equal(f.requests.length, 1);
  f.requests[0].resolve(current); await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  assert.equal(f.state.errors.theme, 'Current error');
});

for (const outcome of ['resolve', 'reject']) {
  test(`save invalidates pending validation ${outcome} after baseline replacement`, async () => {
    const f = await fixture();
    f.state.values.theme = 'dark';
    const pending = f.validate();
    f.scheduleValidation();
    const saving = f.submit();
    assert.equal(f.timers.size, 0);
    assert.equal(f.state.validationTimer, null);
    await f.validate();
    assert.equal(f.requests.length, 1);
    f.saves[0].resolve({ valid: true, values: { theme: 'dark' }, errors: {}, effects: [] });
    await saving;
    assert.equal(f.state.baseline.theme, 'dark');
    const expected = snapshot(f);
    f.requests[0][outcome](outcome === 'resolve' ? stale : new Error('Old failure'));
    await pending;
    assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
  });
}

test('pending validation cannot update UI while save is in flight', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  f.state.values.theme = 'dark';
  const pending = f.validate(), saving = f.submit();
  const expected = snapshot(f);
  f.requests[0].resolve(stale); await pending;
  assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
  f.saves[0].resolve({ valid: false, errors: { theme: 'Save error' } }); await saving;
  assert.equal(f.state.errors.theme, 'Save error');
});

test('reset invalidates pending responses and cancels queued validation', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  f.state.values.theme = 'dark';
  const pending = f.validate();
  f.scheduleValidation(); f.restoreBaseline();
  assert.equal(f.timers.size, 0);
  assert.equal(f.state.values.theme, 'system');
  const expected = snapshot(f);
  f.requests[0].resolve(stale); await pending;
  assert.equal(snapshot(f), expected);
});

test('latest validation rejection still reports its error', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const pending = f.validate();
  f.requests[0].reject(new Error('Current failure')); await pending;
  assert.equal(f.state.errors._form, 'Current failure');
  assert.equal(f.node('form-message').textContent, 'Settings could not be validated.');
});



test('busy covers debounce and validation and clears on rejection', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  f.state.values.theme = 'dark';
  f.scheduleValidation();
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
  assert.equal(f.node('save').disabled, true);
  const pending = f.validate();
  f.requests[0].reject(new Error('Validation failure'));
  await pending;
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
});

test('stale validation cannot clear the newer busy state', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const older = f.validate(), newer = f.validate();
  f.requests[0].resolve(stale); await older;
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
  f.requests[1].resolve(current); await newer;
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
});

test('save announces progress outside the busy fields and focuses after enabling controls', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  f.state.values.theme = 'dark';
  let focused = false, scrolled = false;
  const control = {
    focus() { assert.equal(f.state.saving, false); focused = true; },
    scrollIntoView(options) { assert.equal(options.behavior, 'instant'); scrolled = true; },
  };
  f.node('settings-form').querySelector = selector => {
    assert.equal(selector, '[aria-invalid="true"]:not(:disabled)');
    return control;
  };
  const pending = f.submit();
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
  assert.match(f.node('form-message').textContent, /Validating and saving/);
  f.saves[0].resolve({ valid: false, errors: { theme: 'Invalid' } }); await pending;
  assert.equal(focused, true); assert.equal(scrolled, true);
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
});

test('save rejection focuses the status when no field error is available', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const pending = f.submit();
  f.saves[0].reject(new Error('Disk error')); await pending;
  assert.equal(f.node('form-message').focused, true);
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
});

test('successful save clears busy without moving focus', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const pending = f.submit();
  f.saves[0].resolve({ valid: true, values: { theme: 'dark' }, effects: [] }); await pending;
  assert.equal(f.node('settings-sections').getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
  assert.equal(f.node('form-message').focused, undefined);
});

test('navigation marks exactly one current link and removes the old marker', async () => {
  const f = await fixture();
  const links = ['#section-general', '#section-export'].map(hash => ({
    hash, attributes: {},
    setAttribute(name, value) { this.attributes[name] = value; },

  assert.equal(links[1].attributes['aria-current'], undefined);
});
